**[00:22:00.820] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-000171)  
Mr. Speaker, Madam Speaker, distinguished colleagues, I rise to sponsor the proposed fiscal year 2027 budget of the Department of Social Welfare and Development.

**[00:22:15.200] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-000172)  
The proposed national budget carries the theme, People-Centered Growth for an Inclusive and Resilient Future.

**[00:22:23.640] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-000173)  
Under it, P241.2 billion is proposed for the DSWD to support vulnerable families and provide assistance to those in need.

**[00:22:34.660] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-000174)  
Behind that figure are people who cannot wait, a child who needs a nutritious meal, a family struggling to keep its children in school,

**[00:22:45.200] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-000175)  
an older person with no steady income, and a household rebuilding after a disaster.

**[00:22:51.660] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-000176)  
The DSWD reaches these families at different points in their lives.

**[00:22:59.160] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-000177)  
The Pantawid Pamilyang Pilipino Program supports children's health and education.

**[00:23:04.820] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-000178)  
Feeding and food assistance address hunger.

**[00:23:08.920] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-000179)  
Protective services help people through crisis.

**[00:23:13.240] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-000180)  
Livelihoods offer a path

**[00:23:15.200] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-000181)  
toward greater security.

**[00:23:18.380] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-000182)  
Social pensions help older Filipinos meet their daily needs.

**[00:23:23.980] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-000183)  
These programs are strongest when they work together.

**[00:23:28.480] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-000184)  
Emergency assistance helps a family get through today.

**[00:23:32.700] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-000185)  
Education, nutrition, and livelihood opportunities can help change what tomorrow holds.

**[00:23:39.040] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-000186)  
Madam Speaker, Congress must also ask what this appropriation will achieve.

**[00:23:45.200] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-000187)  
Are eligible families receiving help on time?

**[00:23:49.660] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-000188)  
Are we reaching communities that are hardest to serve, including island and remote barangays?

**[00:23:57.100] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-000189)  
Are our programs helping households recover from hardships and build a more secure future?

**[00:24:06.380] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-000190)  
We owe the Filipino people accurate targeting, timely delivery, transparent spending, and results.

**[00:24:15.200] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-000191)  
They can feel in their daily lives.

**[00:24:18.460] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-000192)  
Secretary Rex Gatchalian reported during the Committee on Appropriations briefing

**[00:24:23.320] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-000193)  
that the Department achieved nearly full utilization of its 2025 funds.

**[00:24:30.020] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-000194)  
That record deserves recognition.

**[00:24:33.160] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-000195)  
It also sets a standard for the year ahead.

**[00:24:36.620] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-000196)  
Funds must be used effectively and assistance must reach the people for whom Congress provides it.

**[00:24:44.020] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-000197)  
The value of this budget

**[00:24:45.200] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-000198)  
will be measured in a child better nourished, a student able to remain in school, a family supported through a crisis, an older person treated with dignity, and a community able to recover after a disaster.

**[00:25:02.640] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-000199)  
Our task is to turn public resources into public good. For social welfare, that means being present when the people need government most and helping families move forward, lasting security and opportunity.

**[00:25:18.440] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-000200)  
I therefore respectfully sponsor the proposed fiscal year 2027 budget of the Department of Social Welfare and Development and urge this Honorable Chamber to give it its careful consideration.

**[00:25:32.640] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-000201)  
Thank you, Madam Speaker.

**[00:25:34.920] Deputy Speaker Mercedes K. Alvarez** (asr-000202)  
Majority Leader.

**[00:25:37.040] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-000203)  
Madam Speaker, I move that we recognize the Honorable Representative Presley de Jesus of Partylist, Phil Reka, to interpolate on the proposed budget of the DSWD and its attached supervisors.

**[00:25:47.440] Deputy Speaker Mercedes K. Alvarez** (asr-000204)  
Representative Presley de Jesus of Phil Reka, Partylist, is hereby recognized for his interpolation.

**[00:25:54.140] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000205)

Thank you, Madam Speaker, and of course, Distinguished Sponsor,

**[01:01:21.640] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000976)  
maraming salamat to the DSWD family.

**[01:01:24.400] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000977)  
I hope the digitalization, digitalization,

**[01:01:29.020] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000978)  
of the beneficiary, sana matupad na natin.

**[01:01:32.760] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000979)  
At the same time, sana po, bigyan pansin natin itong mga centenarians natin, no?

**[01:01:37.220] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000980)  
At sila po ang ating founding fathers na nauna sa atin.

**[01:01:40.200] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000981)  
Sana, huwag na magkaroon ng delay.

**[01:01:42.660] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000982)  
Kasi any delay, nababawasan yung araw nila sa mundo.

**[01:01:46.480] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000983)  
Yun lang po, maraming salamat po sa inyo lahat.

**[01:01:48.540] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000984)  
I support the budget of DSWD family.

**[01:01:51.780] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000985)  
Thank you, Secretary.

**[01:01:52.820] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-000986)  
Thank you, Your Honor, Madam Speaker.

**[01:01:56.020] Deputy Speaker Mercedes K. Alvarez** (asr-000987)  
Majority Leader?

**[01:01:56.820] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-000988)  
Madam Speaker?

**[01:01:59.020] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-000989)  
I move that we recognize Representative J.C. Abalos of 4P's party list for his interpellation.

**[01:02:05.020] Deputy Speaker Mercedes K. Alvarez** (asr-000990)  
Representative J.C. Abalos of the 4P's party list is hereby recognized for his interpellation.

**[01:02:10.020] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-000991)  
Maraming salamat po, Madam Speaker.

**[01:02:12.020] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-000992)  
Good day to our colleagues in this Chamber,

**[01:02:15.020] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-000993)  
to the Distinguished Sponsor,

**[01:02:17.020] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-000994)  
and to the Secretary of the Department of Social Welfare and Development, Secretary Rex,

**[01:02:22.020] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-000995)  
together with the heads of its attached and supervised agency,

**[01:02:26.020] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-000996)  
who are with us as in this session.

**[01:02:28.020] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-000997)  
Before anything else, Madam Speaker,

**[01:02:31.020] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-000998)  
let me place on record my appreciation,

**[01:02:34.020] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-000999)  
and I am certain that this is shared by the Chamber for the work of the Department and its people.

**[01:02:40.020] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001000)  
The 4P's alone has helped 1.77 million households graduate to self-sufficiency since 2022,

**[01:02:48.020] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001001)  
and 7.24 million children of beneficiary households have completed basic education.

**[01:02:55.020] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001002)  
Through the Quick Response Fund,

**[01:02:57.020] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001003)  
and its network of over a thousand warehouses,

**[01:03:00.020] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001004)  
the Department continues to be the first responder of our people in times of disaster.

**[01:03:08.020] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001005)  
This is not a Department we come here to fault.

**[01:03:11.020] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001006)  
This is a Department we come here to strengthen and to support.

**[01:03:14.020] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001007)  
Ngayon, Madam Speaker, para sa una kong katanungan, nalagyan ko po ng konting konteksto.

**[01:03:21.020] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001008)  
Poverty incidents among the population fell to 9.7%.

**[01:03:27.020] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001009)  
In 2025.

**[01:03:29.020] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001010)  
But this figure does not yet reflect the Middle East-linked oil price shock

**[01:03:35.020] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001011)  
projected by PIDS to add up to 1.34 million poor Filipinos,

**[01:03:41.020] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001012)  
and the Philippines' new upper middle-income country classification,

**[01:03:46.020] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001013)  
which may require the poverty line used for cross-country comparison

**[01:03:50.020] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001014)  
to rise from 4.20 dollars a day to 8.3 dollars a day,

**[01:03:56.020] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001015)  
potentially tripling the estimated incidence.

**[01:04:01.020] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001016)  
Ngayon, Madam Speaker,

**[01:04:02.020] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001017)  
given the PIDS projection that the ongoing oil price shock

**[01:04:08.020] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001018)  
could push as many as 1.34 million additional Filipinos into poverty,

**[01:04:14.020] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001019)  
and the possible upward revision of the poverty threshold

**[01:04:18.020] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001020)  
following our recent reclassification as an upper middle-income country,

**[01:04:24.020] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001021)  
was the Department's 2027 budget calibrated against these emerging risks

**[01:04:30.020] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001022)  
or prepared solely on the basis of the 2025 preliminary poverty rate?

**[01:04:36.020] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-001023)  
May we repeat the question again, Madam Speaker, Your Honor?

**[01:04:48.020] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001024)  
Madam Speaker, ayon po sa datos, bumaba daw po ang poverty incidence

**[01:04:52.020] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001025)  
sa ating populasyon to 9.7 percent in 2025.

**[01:04:59.020] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001026)  
Ngunit, Madam Speaker, hindi pa po na-re-reflect sa figure na ito

**[01:05:05.020] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001027)  
ang epekto ng Middle East oil price shock,

**[01:05:09.020] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001028)  
at ayon po sa mga pagsusuri ng PIDS,

**[01:05:13.020] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001029)  
maaaring dumagdag po ang dami ng ating mga kababayan na mahihirap.

**[01:05:18.020] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001030)  
Maaaring dumagdag po ito ng 1.34 million.

**[01:05:20.020] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001031)  
At dahil nagkaroon po ng upper middle-income classification ang ating bansa,

**[01:05:28.020] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001032)  
yung ating threshold for poverty line cross-country comparison

**[01:05:33.020] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001033)  
maaaring umangat from 4.20 dollars a day to 8.30 dollars a day,

**[01:05:38.020] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001034)  
tripling the estimated incidence.

**[01:05:40.020] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001035)  
So ang katanungan ko po, Madam Speaker,

**[01:05:43.020] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001036)  
given the projection ng ating oil price shock,

**[01:05:47.020] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001037)  
at hindi pa po ito na-factor in,

**[01:05:49.020] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001038)  
sa 9.7 percent poverty incidence rate,

**[01:05:52.020] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001039)  
at dahil na-re-classify po tayo to upper middle-income classification,

**[01:05:57.020] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001040)  
gusto ko po malaman kung ang budget ng DSWD for 2027,

**[01:06:02.020] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001041)  
na-calibrate na po ba ito considering these emerging risks?

**[01:06:07.020] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001042)  
Or hinanda po ba siya on the basis of the 2025 preliminary poverty rate?

**[01:06:15.020] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-001043)  
Madam Speaker, first of all, Your Honor,

**[01:06:18.020] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-001044)  
what the figure that is being considered as of the moment

**[01:06:23.020] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-001045)  
is not the current figure which is below 10 percent,

**[01:06:26.020] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-001046)  
but rather the 2023 figure which is at 15 percent.

**[01:06:31.020] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-001047)  
That's the basis.

**[01:06:33.020] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-001048)  
And looking forward to what was mentioned on the current crisis

**[01:06:41.020] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-001049)  
that we are experiencing,

**[01:06:48.020] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-001050)  
AICS was tapped to address this

**[01:06:53.020] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-001051)  
by the program we call Uplift.

**[01:07:00.020] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-001052)  
There's a tap-up as far as four-piece beneficiaries are concerned

**[01:07:09.020] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-001053)  
to further augment their benefits

**[01:07:16.020] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-001054)  
because of this situation, Your Honor, Madam Speaker.

**[01:07:21.020] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001055)  
Madam Speaker, nabanggit po ng ating distinguished sponsor

**[01:07:24.020] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001056)  
na pinagbasihan po natin dito ang poverty incidence rate ng 2023,

**[01:07:29.020] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001057)  
the crafting of the current proposed budget for 2027.

**[01:07:33.020] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001058)  
As I've mentioned, Madam Speaker,

**[01:07:35.020] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001059)  
there are current emerging risks that our country is facing

**[01:07:38.020] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001060)  
aside from the Middle East crisis.

**[01:07:40.020] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001061)  
Of course, meron pa pong inflation among several other problems.

**[01:07:44.020] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001062)  
So ang tanong ko po naman ng kasunodan pong tanong, Madam Speaker,

**[01:07:48.020] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001063)  
should any of these risks materialize within the upcoming fiscal year,

**[01:07:54.020] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001064)  
meron po bang contingency financing plan ang ating departamento

**[01:07:58.020] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001065)  
kung sakaling mapatunayan na ang nararanasan ng ating mga kababayan sa ground

**[01:08:03.020] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001066)  
ay mas manubha pa po kumpara noong taong 2023?

**[01:08:06.020] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-001067)  
As what we have done for this year,

**[01:08:09.020] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-001068)  
AICS was tapped to augment

**[01:08:13.020] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-001069)  
the benefits that the 4Ps beneficiaries are receiving

**[01:08:19.020] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-001070)  
under the program Uplift.

**[01:08:22.020] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-001071)  
The possibility of having such again

**[01:08:27.020] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-001072)  
would certainly be, I think, a repeat of the intervention may be done

**[01:08:36.020] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-001073)  
to address the situation again in case there is another crisis

**[01:08:40.020] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-001074)  
or for that matter, Madam Speaker.

**[01:08:43.020] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001075)  
For my next set of questions, Madam Speaker,

**[01:08:46.020] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001076)  
may mga katanungan lang rin naman po ako tungkol naman po sa ating four-piece program.

**[01:08:51.020] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001077)  
Tulad ng nabanggit po kanina ni Congressman Presley,

**[01:08:55.020] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001078)  
may nabanggit po siya kung saan may mga pagkakataon na maaaring nagkaroon po ng onting overpayments

**[01:09:01.020] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001079)  
tuwing nagkakaroon ng delisting delays.

**[01:09:03.020] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001080)  
And nauunawaan ko po ang problema ito ng departamento dahil nakita ko rin po mismo

**[01:09:09.020] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001081)  
at natuklasan ko po mismo on the ground,

**[01:09:12.020] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001082)  
na may mga pagkakataon kung saan malaki po yung caseload ng ating mga social worker

**[01:09:17.020] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001083)  
kaya hindi po maiwasan na magkakaroon po tayo ng mga delays.

**[01:09:21.020] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001084)  
On top of that, mayroon rin delays sa CBMS

**[01:09:24.020] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001085)  
kaya may mga times na delayed po talaga ang ating delisting.

**[01:09:29.020] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001086)  
So, ako po, Madam Speaker, gusto ko pong malaman kung ano po ang mga hakbang na ginagawa ng ating departamento.

**[01:09:39.020] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001087)  
Meron po ba tayong mga...

**[01:09:42.020] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001088)  
mechanisms or system upgrades para maiwasan po ang mga instances of delays

**[01:09:49.020] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001089)  
pagdating po sa delisting ng mga households na nakita na nating self-sufficient or for whatever reasons as the case may be.

**[01:09:56.020] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-001090)  
As mentioned earlier, Madam Speaker, Your Honor,

**[01:09:59.020] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-001091)  
the department is working on a new program that would provide almost a real-time tracking system.

**[01:10:10.020] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-001092)  
In this case,

**[01:10:11.020] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-001093)  
concerns on those mentioned will be outrightly addressed through this digitalization program, Your Honor, Madam Speaker.

**[01:10:23.020] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001094)  
Madam Speaker, nagpapasalamat po ako sa ating departamento dahil mag-i-institutionalize tayo ng real-time tagging system

**[01:10:30.020] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001095)  
para maiwasan po ang delays sa pagdelist ng mga beneficiaries na kailangan na pong graduate.

**[01:10:38.020] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001096)  
And on top of this question,

**[01:10:39.020] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001097)  
naalala ko nga po, Madam Speaker,

**[01:10:40.020] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001098)  
noong 19th Congress,

**[01:10:43.020] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001099)  
nakasama po namin ang department nung inaaral namin ang posibilidad

**[01:10:48.020] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001100)  
na i-empower naman po rin ang mga parent leader naman ng 4Ps.

**[01:10:52.020] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001101)  
Dahil for the longest time, tulad ng banggit ko po kanina,

**[01:10:55.020] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001102)  
ang ating mga city and municipal link,

**[01:10:58.020] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001103)  
napakarami po na households ang kanilang inaasikaso.

**[01:11:02.020] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001104)  
If I remember correctly,

**[01:11:04.020] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001105)  
it can go as high as, a city or municipal link could cater to around

**[01:11:08.020] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001106)  
more than 500 households in some instances.

**[01:11:13.020] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001107)  
At naniniwala po ako na siguro bumaba na po yung numero na yun.

**[01:11:16.020] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001108)  
Gusto ko po malaman ngayon kung ano yung stance ng department pagdating po sa aming panukala

**[01:11:24.020] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001109)  
na i-empower po yung mga parent leader para matulungan po yung department

**[01:11:28.020] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001110)  
sa pag-verify ng mga households na kailangan ng graduate mula sa programa.

**[01:11:33.020] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001111)  
And of course, kinikilala rin po ng departamento sa kanilang IRR yung kakalagahan.

**[01:11:38.020] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001112)  
ng mga parent leader ng mga 4Ps.

**[01:11:41.020] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-001113)  
The proposal, Your Honor, Madam Speaker, is very much acceptable to the department.

**[01:11:47.020] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-001114)  
Fortunately, the department cannot compensate these leaders as well.

**[01:11:57.020] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-001115)  
There's no additional budget for that.

**[01:12:00.020] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001116)  
Maraming maraming salamat po, Mr. Distinguished Sponsor,

**[01:12:03.020] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001117)  
dahil nananatili pa rin po ang suporta ng departamento.

**[01:12:07.020] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001118)  
At kinikilala pa rin nila ang kahalagan ng mga parent leader ng 4Ps

**[01:12:11.020] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001119)  
pagdating po sa pagpaunlad ng ating programa.

**[01:12:15.020] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001120)  
Ngayon, Madam Speaker, may mga katanungan rin po ako

**[01:12:18.020] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001121)  
pagdating naman po sa status ng mga wait-listed households.

**[01:12:22.020] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001122)  
Dahil alam naman po nating lahat, noong nag-transition ang listahanan to CBMS,

**[01:12:31.020] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001123)  
may mga households po sa CBMS na hindi mahanap sa listahanan.

**[01:12:35.020] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001124)  
Ayon po.

**[01:12:36.020] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001125)  
Ayon po sa datos na meron ako dito,

**[01:12:39.020] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001126)  
pagdating po sa qualified set 12 beneficiaries,

**[01:12:43.020] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001127)  
nagkaroon po ng as much as 434,069 households who were wait-listed.

**[01:12:50.020] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001128)  
166,251 were endorsed by the Social Registry Office to be included in the 4Ps CBMS.

**[01:13:00.020] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001129)  
So yung tanong ko po, Madam Speaker,

**[01:13:02.020] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001130)  
dito sa mga wait-listed households on the 4Ps,

**[01:13:04.020] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001131)  
dito sa mga wait-listed households under set 12,

**[01:13:07.020] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001132)  
ilan na lang po dito ang natitira for special revalidation?

**[01:13:13.020] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-001133)  
For all those wait-listed,

**[01:13:18.020] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-001134)  
the 434,069, only 59,462 were not identified as of yet.

**[01:13:31.020] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-001135)  
So the rest are already in.

**[01:13:34.020] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-001136)  
And will be part of the 3.5 million beneficiaries for next year.

**[01:13:41.020] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001137)  
Madam Speaker, pwede ko rin po bang malaman kung yung 59,000 households nito,

**[01:13:46.020] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001138)  
may certain areas ba kung saan concentrated yung population ng mga households na ito?

**[01:13:53.020] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001139)  
Dahil maaaring nasa geographic areas sila na mahirap abutin,

**[01:13:58.020] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001140)  
kaya siguro mahirap ma-validate.

**[01:14:00.020] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001141)  
So, naka-curious po ako, Madam Speaker, kung saan po yung,

**[01:14:03.020] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001142)  
naka-concentrate yung numero na ito?

**[01:14:06.020] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001143)  
And if I may also ask, Madam Speaker,

**[01:14:09.020] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001144)  
gaano na po katagal naghihintay ang mga households na wait-listed for special revalidation?

**[01:14:33.020] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-001145)  
So the validation process started early part of this year.

**[01:14:49.020] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-001146)  
And the number or the 59,000 is spread all over.

**[01:14:55.020] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-001147)  
And they were, I think, advice was given.

**[01:15:00.560] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-001148)  
that the first thing that they can do is to approach their respective local government units

**[01:15:06.800] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-001149)  
for purposes of identification and validation, Your Honor, Madam Speaker.

**[01:15:12.660] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001150)  
Madam Speaker, if I may also ask, did the Philippine Statistics Authority commit to a certain target timeline

**[01:15:20.180] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001151)  
kung kailan po nila maaaring matapos ang kanilang special validation ng mga 59,000 households?

**[01:15:26.840] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-001152)  
They committed. Unfortunately, they're short of the target, Your Honor, Madam Speaker.

**[01:15:33.680] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001153)  
My question, Madam Speaker, is is there a specific target date in mind?

**[01:15:39.500] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001154)  
Kailan po kaya, when is the department expecting the PSA to complete this?

**[01:15:45.820] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001155)  
I understand po dahil iba po yung trabaho ng departamento sa PSA

**[01:15:49.840] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001156)  
at nagtutulungan lamang po ang PSA at ng DSWD pagdating sa special validation.

**[01:15:56.840] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001157)  
So yung tanong ko lang po, may kinamit po kaya ang PSA sa DSWD kung kailan matatapos ang validation?

**[01:16:03.180] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-001158)  
They informed the department that they will be done with this by end of September, Your Honor.

**[01:16:10.220] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001159)  
By end of September. Maraming salamat po, Madam Speaker.

**[01:16:13.640] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001160)  
And then for my next question, nakasanayan na po kasi and I think it's been a practice for quite some time

**[01:16:20.120] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001161)  
na yung target coverage ng 4Ps ay pumapalo ng mga 4.4 million households.

**[01:16:26.840] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001162)  
And natuklasan po natin na beginning 2026, naging 3.5 million po ang ating target.

**[01:16:35.000] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001163)  
So maaari ko po ba malaman kung ano po yung basis natin for this reduced target under the proposed 2027 budget?

**[01:16:46.580] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-001164)  
There's a continuous decrease in the poverty incidence, Your Honor, Madam Speaker.

**[01:16:53.500] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-001165)  
The very reason why the targeted beneficiary.

**[01:16:56.840] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-001166)  
That is also lowered down.

**[01:16:58.980] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-001167)  
Despite the decrease in the poverty incidence,

**[01:17:04.960] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-001168)  
the department still identified the 10% of the near poor group to be covered under this program.

**[01:17:15.880] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-001169)  
So it's not totally poorest,

**[01:17:18.560] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-001170)  
but the same way, a certain percentage of the near poor, Your Honor, Madam Speaker.

**[01:17:26.840] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001171)  
So is it safe to say, Madam Speaker, that the basis for lowering the target number of households for the 4Ps program

**[01:17:33.720] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001172)  
is based on the prevailing poverty incidence rate?

**[01:17:37.780] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-001173)  
Yes, Your Honor, Madam Speaker.

**[01:17:40.820] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-001174)  
Madam Speaker, para sa...

**[01:17:42.520] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-001175)  
At 15%.

**[01:17:44.020] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001176)  
Yes, 15% yung basis po.

**[01:17:46.620] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001177)  
And then, if I may ask, Madam Speaker, is the poverty incidence rate typically based on the family income expenditure survey?

**[01:17:56.760] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001178)  
Conducted by the PSA?

**[01:17:58.180] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001179)  
Ano po kaya yung nagiging pinaka-statistical basis kapag kinukomplete po natin ito?

**[01:18:03.780] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-001180)  
It's the PSA process, Your Honor, Madam Speaker,

**[01:18:12.640] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-001181)  
that they utilize the family income and expenditure survey.

**[01:18:17.440] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001182)  
Madam Speaker, base po sa aking pananaliksik,

**[01:18:21.400] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001183)  
natuklas na rin po natin na yung family income expenditure survey

**[01:18:25.140] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001184)  
is sinasagawa po ito.

**[01:18:26.760] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001185)  
Every two years.

**[01:18:28.940] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001186)  
And maybe two or three years.

**[01:18:31.180] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001187)  
Please correct me if I'm wrong.

**[01:18:32.440] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-001188)  
Every three years.

**[01:18:33.680] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001189)  
And tulad po ng CBMS, tulad rin po ng family income expenditure survey,

**[01:18:38.400] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001190)  
dahil meron po tayong three-year cycle na sinusundan,

**[01:18:41.820] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001191)  
ang pangamba po ng representasyong ito ay

**[01:18:44.640] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001192)  
kung ito po ang ating pagbabasihan,

**[01:18:48.600] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001193)  
marami po kasing pwede mangyari sa loob ng tatlong taon.

**[01:18:51.660] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001194)  
Hindi po natin alam kung magkakaroon ng isa pang pandemia

**[01:18:54.960] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001195)  
o isa pang crisis,

**[01:18:56.760] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001196)  
tungkol sa Middle East oil crisis.

**[01:18:59.460] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001197)  
For example,

**[01:19:01.100] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001198)  
so what I'm trying to say, Madam Speaker,

**[01:19:04.200] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001199)  
is that pagamat maganda na nagkakaroon po tayo ng mga census

**[01:19:09.100] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001200)  
and ng mga survey para imeasure kung ilan sa ating mga kababayan

**[01:19:14.020] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001201)  
ang nakakaranas ng kahirapan,

**[01:19:16.560] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001202)  
marami pong pwedeng mangyari in a span of three years.

**[01:19:19.180] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001203)  
And we always want the budget to reflect the existing realities

**[01:19:23.640] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001204)  
that happen on the ground.

**[01:19:25.260] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001205)  
Kaya nabanggit ko po kanina,

**[01:19:26.760] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001206)  
Madam Speaker,

**[01:19:27.900] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001207)  
na hindi pa natin natatake into account

**[01:19:30.700] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001208)  
yung Middle East crisis,

**[01:19:33.200] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001209)  
yung oil price shock,

**[01:19:35.440] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001210)  
yung inflation.

**[01:19:36.720] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001211)  
So gusto ko lang po sana hingiin yung commitment

**[01:19:39.300] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001212)  
ng ating sponsor at ng ating department

**[01:19:41.860] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001213)  
na mag-communicate constantly, of course,

**[01:19:46.260] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001214)  
with the DBM and the DepDev as the case may be

**[01:19:49.640] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001215)  
kung sakaling nakita po nila on the ground

**[01:19:52.020] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001216)  
na malaki na po ang impact ng mga problemang ito.

**[01:19:55.940] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001217)  
Considering na hindi po siya factored in

**[01:19:58.060] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001218)  
sa planning stage po ng ating budget.

**[01:20:01.160] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-001219)  
The department agrees to that, Madam Speaker, Your Honor.

**[01:20:05.700] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-001220)  
In fact, we have the assistance for individuals in crisis situation

**[01:20:16.680] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-001221)  
which usually the immediate program that can address

**[01:20:24.200] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-001222)  
temporary relief.

**[01:20:25.940] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-001223)  
For these cases.

**[01:20:27.940] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-001224)  
And the department can as well come up with temporary programs

**[01:20:33.940] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-001225)  
like what they have done as far as uplift is concerned,

**[01:20:37.940] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-001226)  
Your Honor, Madam Speaker.

**[01:20:39.940] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-001227)  
And that could be duplicated or scaled up just if necessary,

**[01:20:46.940] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-001228)  
I suppose, Madam Speaker, Your Honor.

**[01:20:48.940] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001229)  
Madam Speaker, gusto ko rin po ipaalam sa ating distinguished sponsor at sa department,

**[01:20:54.940] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001230)  
at sigurado po akong

**[01:20:55.940] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001231)  
alam ito ng department

**[01:20:57.940] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001232)  
na napasa na po ng mababang kapulungan

**[01:21:00.940] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001233)  
ang ledak priority measure na pagtibayan pa po ang Four-Peace Act.

**[01:21:05.940] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001234)  
And dito ini-institutionalize po natin ang first 1,000 days,

**[01:21:09.940] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001235)  
ang rice subsidy, for example.

**[01:21:12.940] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001236)  
So gusto ko lang po itanong sa ating sponsor at sa ating department na

**[01:21:16.940] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001237)  
considering na may onting pagbawas sa pondo ng Four-Peace program,

**[01:21:22.940] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001238)  
intact pa rin po ba ang mga programang ito

**[01:21:25.940] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001239)  
para sa ating mga kababayan?

**[01:21:26.940] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-001240)  
As far as Four-Peace, Your Honor, Madam Speaker,

**[01:21:29.940] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-001241)  
it's still sufficient considering that the number targeted is smaller than what we have right now,

**[01:21:37.940] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-001242)  
currently here.

**[01:21:39.940] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001243)  
And then, Madam Speaker, for my next set of questions,

**[01:21:42.940] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001244)  
tatanong naman po ako tungkol sa Supplemental Feeding Program and Child Nutrition.

**[01:21:47.940] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001245)  
Performance data show mixed results.

**[01:21:49.940] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001246)  
The percentage of malnourished children with improved nutritional status reached

**[01:21:54.940] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001247)  
78.92% against a 70.05% target in 2025.

**[01:22:00.940] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001248)  
But only 61,371 of an expected higher cohort were confirmed served

**[01:22:08.940] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001249)  
under the tighter 2025 budget window.

**[01:22:12.940] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001250)  
Madam Speaker, the reduction of SFP funding by a third appears to walk back

**[01:22:18.940] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001251)  
the extended 180 feeding cycle achieved in 2026.

**[01:22:24.940] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001252)  
Can the good sponsor confirm whether reverting toward a 120-day cycle

**[01:22:30.940] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001253)  
for 1.9 million children is expected to affect nutritional outcomes

**[01:22:36.940] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001254)  
and how this squares with our national nutrition policy?

**[01:22:44.940] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-001255)  
Technically, it will be much better for the department if the 2026 level

**[01:22:52.940] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-001256)  
can be restored for 2027, Madam Speaker, Your Honor.

**[01:22:56.940] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001257)  
So, Madam Speaker, with the reduced allocation,

**[01:23:00.940] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001258)  
may I know how many feeding days can the DSWD sustain?

**[01:23:04.940] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001259)  
And if it's possible for us to maintain the 180-day cycle achieved in 2026,

**[01:23:10.940] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001260)  
or would funding constraints require a return to 120 days?

**[01:23:16.940] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-001261)  
The estimate goes down to 120 days, Your Honor, Madam Speaker.

**[01:23:20.940] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001262)  
Maraming salamat po, Mr. Distinguished Sponsor.

**[01:23:24.940] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001263)  
Para sa aking susunod naman pong katanungan, gusto ko lang pong ipaalam

**[01:23:30.940] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001264)  
sa departamento ang aking mga obserbasyon po pagdating po sa CBMS.

**[01:23:34.940] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001265)  
Alam naman po nating lahat, napakahalaga ng CBMS dahil

**[01:23:40.940] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001266)  
nagge-generate po tayo ng aggregated and disaggregated data at the household level,

**[01:23:46.940] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001267)  
at nagsisilbi po itong gabay

**[01:23:48.940] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001268)  
hindi lamang po sa mga lokal na pamahalaan kung hindi lalong-lalo na po sa ating national programs.

**[01:23:56.940] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001269)  
So, this year, we learned from the Walang Gutom program

**[01:24:02.940] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001270)  
that the DSWD notified the PSA that we have 238 households

**[01:24:10.940] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001271)  
with unidentified or disputed roster members,

**[01:24:14.940] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001272)  
and 128 households that their LGUs,

**[01:24:18.940] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001273)  
that LGUs confirmed are no longer residing in the area surveyed.

**[01:24:22.940] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001274)  
Are these figures drawn from the full Walang Gutom program beneficiary list

**[01:24:26.940] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001275)  
or only a sample from a specific region?

**[01:24:34.940] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-001276)  
It's the whole, Your Honor, Madam Speaker.

**[01:24:36.940] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001277)  
Madam Speaker, gusto ko po malaman na kung ano po ang mga,

**[01:24:42.940] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001278)  
kung nagkaroon po ba ng talakayan ang DSWD, pati po yung PSA,

**[01:24:46.940] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001279)  
para maiwasan po yung discrepancies sa pag-identify ng mga beneficiaries

**[01:24:52.940] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001280)  
na hindi pala dapat nasasali doon.

**[01:24:56.940] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001281)  
Dahil naiintindihan ko po na ang pinagbabasihan lamang ng DSWD

**[01:25:00.940] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001282)  
ay ang ibinibigay po sa kanila ng ating CBMS.

**[01:25:04.940] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-001283)  
That's why the department, Madam Speaker, Your Honor,

**[01:25:08.940] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-001284)  
continues with the validation process in the field.

**[01:25:12.940] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-001285)  
There's always an ongoing validation.

**[01:25:16.940] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-001286)  
As far as confirming or final acceptance of beneficiaries,

**[01:25:26.940] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-001287)  
Your Honor, Madam Speaker.

**[01:25:28.940] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001288)  
For my next question, Madam Speaker, gusto ko po itanong rin.

**[01:25:32.940] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001289)  
Tungkol naman po ito sa mga contract of service and job order personnel ng DSWD,

**[01:25:38.940] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001290)  
particularly po dito sa mga ating four-piece links.

**[01:25:42.940] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001291)  
With over 17,000 contract of service and job order personnel,

**[01:25:44.940] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001292)  
more than triple the department's authorized permanent positions,

**[01:25:50.940] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001293)  
many of whom have served as four-piece links for over a decade,

**[01:25:54.940] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001294)  
when will the DBM-approved 5,627-item enhanced structure

**[01:26:00.940] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001295)  
be reflected in the staffing summary and funded,

**[01:26:04.940] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001296)  
so that frontline social workers may finally be given security of tenure?

**[01:26:12.940] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-001297)  
There's a total of about 5,000 new items for this year, Madam Speaker.

**[01:26:20.940] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-001298)  
And the department is on the process of filling up these new items.

**[01:26:24.940] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-001299)  
And it is expected another 5,000 will be up for next year again, Madam Speaker, Your Honor.

**[01:26:30.940] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001300)  
This is excellent news, Madam Speaker.

**[01:26:32.940] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001301)  
And I commend the department dahil binibigyan po natin ng security of tenure

**[01:26:37.940] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001302)  
ang ating mga social workers na matagal na pong nagsisilbe.

**[01:26:42.940] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001303)  
Para po sa ahensya.

**[01:26:44.940] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001304)  
Ngayon, for my final question po, Madam Speaker,

**[01:26:48.940] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001305)  
may tanong lang po ako tungkol sa Executive Order No. 51, Series of 2023,

**[01:26:54.940] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001306)  
creating the Special Committee on LGBTQIA plus Affairs under the DSWD.

**[01:27:02.940] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001307)  
Yung tanong ko lang po, Madam Speaker, mula nung itinayo ang Special Committee na ito,

**[01:27:08.940] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001308)  
sigurado po ako na marami na pong programa,

**[01:27:12.940] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001309)  
at proyekto, at marami po itong mungkahing activities para pagsilbihan po ang community.

**[01:27:20.940] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001310)  
At nung tinitingnan ko po ang General Appropriations Act,

**[01:27:24.940] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001311)  
wala po ako nakitang item doon kung ano po ang kanilang gagamitan ng pondo

**[01:27:30.940] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001312)  
para mapondohan po ang kanilang mga activity.

**[01:27:34.940] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001313)  
Gusto ko lang po malaman kung paano po kaya natin matutulungan ang Special Committee na ito

**[01:27:40.940] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-001314)  
para magambanan po nila ang kanilang layunin.

kinikilala

po ng butihing

through the

**[01:37:52.080] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001530)

**[01:37:59.960] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001532)

**[01:38:05.960] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001533)  
Moving on, Madam Speaker.

**[01:38:08.960] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001534)  
May recent statement po si BSP Governor Eli Remolona.

**[01:38:20.140] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001535)  
Ang banggit po ni BSP Governor, Madam Speaker,

**[01:38:25.660] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001536)  
kaya daw po halos mawalang savings.

**[01:38:32.260] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001537)  
Basically, if we're going to paraphrase it,

**[01:38:35.860] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001538)  
bakit mahirap ang sitwasyon ng mga pamilya,

**[01:38:38.960] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001539)  
kasi daw Madam Speaker, Mr. Sponsor, mayabang daw ang mga Pilipino.

**[01:38:45.480] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001540)  
Palagastos daw ang mga Pilipino.

**[01:38:49.400] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001541)

**[01:38:58.560] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001542)  
Earning 52 million pesos at least per year.

**[01:39:05.940] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001543)  
So, what would be the position?

**[01:39:08.960] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001544)  
What would be the position of DSWD through the sponsor regarding this irresponsible statement?

**[01:39:19.200] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001545)  
Would the DSWD through the sponsor agree that in its own experience and data available,

**[01:39:29.480] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001546)  
eh hindi naman ang problema yung paggastos, kundi Pilipinos are not really earning well.

**[01:39:35.800] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001547)  
So, it's not a question of them consuming more.

**[01:39:38.960] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001548)

**[01:39:43.200] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001549)

**[01:39:49.580] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-001550)

**[01:39:53.940] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-001551)

**[01:40:00.920] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-001552)

**[01:40:08.960] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-001553)

**[01:40:11.340] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001554)

**[01:40:18.940] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001555)

**[01:40:32.640] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001556)

**[01:40:38.960] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001557)

**[01:40:43.500] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001558)

**[01:40:52.360] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001559)

**[02:00:58.180] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-002031)

**[02:01:11.820] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002032)  
Maraming salamat, Madam Speaker.

**[02:01:13.400] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002033)  
At sa inyo pong pinakahuli na pagpupulong ng uplift, napag-usapan po ba ang tungkol sa

**[02:01:19.500] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002034)  
posible na suspension at pagbabawas ng excise taxes sa petroleum products?

**[02:01:24.900] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002035)  
At ano po ba ang posisyon dito ng DSWD?

**[02:01:28.220] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002036)  
Nakita po natin na nanggaling na rin po sa inyo na hindi lang po ang mga pinaka-mahihirap ang pinaka-apektado.

**[02:01:35.560] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002037)  
Nandyan din ang mga near-poor, ang struggling natin na middle class dahil po sa epekto ng mataas na presyo ng langis.

**[02:01:43.120] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002038)  
Maari ba natin malaman kung merong tindig? May posisyon po ba ang DSWD hinggil sa pagbabawas?

**[02:01:49.500] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002039)  
O yung temporary suspension ng ating excise taxes sa petroleum products, lalong-lalo na po sa diesel at gasoline.

**[02:01:57.940] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002040)  
Sa ngayon po kasi, ang sinuspindi lang na anunsyo ay para lang sa LPG at kerosene, pero hindi po sakop yung diesel at gasolina na lubos na ginagamit ng ating transport sector,

**[02:02:12.220] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002041)  
mga manggagawa, mga magsasaka, mga manginisda, at iba pang affected sectors.

**[02:02:19.500] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-002042)  
Madam Speaker, Your Honor, the Department regrets to address that issue.

**[02:02:24.140] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-002043)  
I would rather refer such to the concerned agency.

**[02:02:29.480] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-002044)  
But the Department is very much committed from its part to address those that are in need in this time of crisis.

**[02:02:41.680] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002045)  
Maraming salamat. Madam Speaker, narinig po natin na milyon-milyon yung naaabot ng mga dagdag tulong sa panahon ng krisis.

**[02:02:48.740] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002046)  
Ngunit ngayon po, sa ilalim ng National Energy Emergency, mga nga ilangan ng mas malawak na sakop ang mga pantulong sa ating mga kababayan,

**[02:03:03.040] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002047)  
lalong-lalo na sa panahon ngayon na hindi lang po mga baralita, kundi marami po ang ating mga kababayan na lubos na dumadain sa hirap ng buhay,

**[02:03:14.620] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002048)  
sa taas ng presyo ng bilihin, na ang laki po ng epekto.

**[02:03:18.740] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002049)  
ng pagkataas ng presyo ng lanis.

**[02:03:21.080] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002050)  
Ngayon po, tungkol naman sa iba na mahalagang nausapin.

**[02:03:24.920] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002051)  
Ang Pilipinas po ay nagratipika ng Hague Convention on the International Recovery of Child Support and Other Forms of Family Maintenance sa taong 2022.

**[02:03:35.680] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002052)  
At nagagalak po tayo na ang DSWD ang natakda na Philippine Central Authority tungkol dito.

**[02:03:44.820] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002053)  
Mahalaga ito sa paghabol ng pagliligay.

**[02:03:48.740] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002054)  
Itiyak na yung mga request at yung mga kaso na incoming at outgoing na may kinalaman sa child support

**[02:03:56.640] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002055)  
at paghingi pa na ibang forma ng family maintenance support ay natitiyak natin na may tulong yung ating mga kababayan.

**[02:04:04.740] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002056)  
Maari po bang malaman kung ano na ang status ng trabaho ng ating International Child Support Secretariat?

**[02:04:15.560] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002057)  
Ito po ba ay may sapat na pondo na kinakailangan?

**[02:04:18.740] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002058)  
Kailangan para matugunan yung mga kaso na may kinalaman sa mga request for international child support.

**[02:04:36.860] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-002059)  
So after the ratification, the concerned agency has been doing it for a while.

**[02:04:48.740] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-002060)  
For the past three years.

**[02:04:59.760] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-002061)  
And at least about 100 cases for child support have been addressed by the agency.

**[02:05:12.380] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-002062)  
Cross-border and both incoming and outgoing.

**[02:05:17.680] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-002063)  
Your Honor, Madam Speaker.

**[02:05:18.740] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002064)  
Sapat po ba sa ngayon ang ating mga personel?

**[02:05:23.160] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002065)  
Meron ba tayong office na maaaring puntahan?

**[02:05:28.000] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002066)  
Ano po ba yung request para sa susunod na taon para matiyak na tuloy-tuloy ang trabaho,

**[02:05:33.840] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002067)  
ang serbisyo na may kinalaman sa pagpunan ng ating obligasyon sa ilalim ng nasabing convention?

**[02:06:08.560] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-002070)  
So the Department of Social Welfare is doing it through its central authority.

**[02:06:23.520] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-002083)  
Yeah, there's a secretariat dedicated for that matter, Your Honor, Madam Speaker.

**[02:06:26.240] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002084)  
Madam Speaker, hingi na lang po tayo ng status ng implementation ng DSWD bilang Philippine Central Authority sa konbensyon na ito.

**[02:06:36.000] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002085)  
At yung detailya na rin po kung meron tayong coordination, kung meron po tayong convergence programs sa iba pong mga departamento

**[02:06:43.200] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002086)  
para matiyak po natin yung ganap na pagtugon sa obligasyon sa nasabing konbensyon.

**[02:06:51.960] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002087)  
Ngayon naman po sa pagharap dito sa kapulungan na ito ng CHR, na ibahagi po nila yung mga nakakabahala na kaso ng abuso at torture sa mga bata, sa mga bahay pag-asa.

**[02:07:08.960] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002088)  
Nalaman na po ba ito ng DSWD? Ano po ang aksyon na ating ginagamit?

**[02:07:13.200] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002089)  
Meron na ba itong sinagawa? Meron na bang investigasyon? At meron na ba tayong napanagot pagdating dito?

**[02:07:19.440] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002090)  
Nakakabahala po ang kanilang naging ulat at nais ko lang pong basahin para sa record natin.

**[02:07:28.180] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002091)  
Nito pong Abril ng taong ito, ang CHR po nag-flag ng seryosong mga gaps na kailangan punan sa implementation ng Juvenile Justice and Welfare Act.

**[02:07:40.200] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002092)  
At isa po dito ay may kinalamang,

**[02:07:43.200] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002093)  
dun sa mga kaso ng abuso sa loob mismo ng mga bahay pag-asa facilities.

**[02:07:49.600] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002094)

**[02:50:51.160] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002938)  
another increase?

**[02:50:53.540] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002939)  
No?

**[02:50:55.200] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-002940)  
The target is to

**[02:50:57.540] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-002941)  
realize a universal

**[02:51:00.760] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-002942)  
social pension, Your Honor, Madam Speaker.

**[02:51:03.220] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002943)  
Yes. Okay.

**[02:51:04.500] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002944)  
Expand the coverage.

**[02:51:06.080] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-002945)  
Expand the coverage.

**[02:51:07.800] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002946)  
Pero, baka, hindi po ba kasama sa target

**[02:51:11.000] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002947)

**[02:51:13.600] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002948)  
Kasi yung 1,000 a month po

**[02:51:15.140] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002949)  
ay kapos na kapos.

**[02:51:17.920] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002950)  
No?

**[02:51:18.960] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-002951)  
Hopefully, if we reach

**[02:51:21.340] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-002952)

**[02:51:23.640] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-002953)

**[02:51:25.040] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-002954)  
increasing again the...

**[02:51:26.720] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002955)  
Magkano ang kailangan for universal coverage?

**[02:51:29.900] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002956)  
And how many

**[02:51:30.680] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002957)  
additional beneficiaries

**[02:51:32.740] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002958)  
would be needed for universal

**[02:51:34.980] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002959)  
coverage?

**[02:51:40.820] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-002960)  
Most likely,

**[02:51:42.540] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-002961)  
double the existing amount.

**[02:51:44.400] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002962)

**[02:51:46.920] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-002963)  
I mean, if we try to

**[02:51:49.100] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-002964)

**[02:51:51.040] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002965)

**[02:51:53.320] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-002966)  
That would double.

**[02:51:55.040] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-002967)  
Most likely, double the amount.

**[02:51:57.540] Deputy Speaker Mercedes K. Alvarez** (asr-002968)  
Kindly wrap up, Representative Dino.

**[02:51:59.480] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002969)  
Ilan po ang

**[02:52:00.260] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002970)  
for universal coverage

**[02:52:02.920] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002971)  
of social pension? Pag sinabi pong universal,

**[02:52:05.080] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002972)

**[02:52:07.240] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002973)  
Yun po ba?

**[02:52:08.520] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-002974)  
Lagay na natin around 10 million.

**[02:52:12.560] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-002975)  
Additional of 10 billion, maybe.

**[02:52:16.220] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-002976)  
A 10 million more

**[02:52:19.800] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-002977)  
beneficiaries to

**[02:52:22.420] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-002978)  
fully realize

**[02:52:24.300] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-002979)  
universal coverage.

**[02:52:24.580] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002980)  
10 million.

**[02:52:24.820] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002981)  
10 million more beneficiaries.

**[02:52:26.620] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002982)  
So, yun po ang target.

**[02:52:27.960] Deputy Speaker Mercedes K. Alvarez** (asr-002983)  
The Chair would like to request, Representative Dino, to wrap up.

**[02:52:31.060] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-002984)  
That is the target.

**[02:52:32.200] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002985)  
Okay.

**[02:52:34.420] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002986)  
Well, Madam Speaker, humihingi ng 30 billion

**[02:52:36.800] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002987)  
para sa dagdag na AICs,

**[02:52:39.600] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002988)  
pero wala pong

**[02:52:41.060] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002989)  
hinihingi para sa dagdag sa

**[02:52:42.920] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002990)  
4 na milyong mga

**[02:52:44.460] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002991)  
senior citizens.

**[02:52:47.540] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002992)  
So, yun po ang isang concern natin

**[02:52:49.420] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002993)  
at baka makonsider

**[02:52:51.020] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002994)  
ng Committee on Appropriations

**[02:52:52.700] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002995)  
na yung 1,000, kahit maging

**[02:52:54.820] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002996)  
1,500, for the

**[02:52:56.740] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002997)  
4 million citizens.

**[02:52:58.060] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-002998)  
Madam Speaker, Your Honor, the Department requested

**[02:53:00.700] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-002999)  
for additional

**[02:53:01.940] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-003000)  
funds for the

**[02:53:04.800] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-003001)

**[02:53:06.080] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003002)  
Okay. So, sana po ay ma-irehistro

**[02:53:09.440] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003003)

**[02:53:11.280] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003004)  
sa

**[02:53:11.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003005)

**[02:53:17.040] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003007)  
Hanggang doon na lamang po, Madam Speaker,

**[02:53:19.300] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003008)

**[03:01:54.000] Rep. Arnan C. Panaligan** (asr-003109)

and Executive Director

**[03:02:04.000] Deputy Speaker Mercedes K. Alvarez** (asr-003112)  
We acknowledge the presence of the heads and officials from the Department of Health,

**[03:02:08.000] Deputy Speaker Mercedes K. Alvarez** (asr-003113)  
including attached agencies and corporations headed by the Secretary Edwin Mercado.

**[03:02:13.000] Deputy Speaker Mercedes K. Alvarez** (asr-003114)  
Welcome to the House of Representatives.

**[03:02:15.000] Deputy Speaker Mercedes K. Alvarez** (asr-003115)  
Majority Leader.

**[03:02:16.000] Rep. Arnan C. Panaligan** (asr-003116)  
Madam Speaker, I move that we open the period of sponsorship and debate.

**[03:02:22.000] Deputy Speaker Mercedes K. Alvarez** (asr-003117)  
Is there any objection? The Chair hears none. The period of sponsorship and debate is now open.

**[03:02:27.000] Deputy Speaker Mercedes K. Alvarez** (asr-003118)  
Majority Leader.

**[03:02:28.000] Rep. Arnan C. Panaligan** (asr-003119)  
Madam Speaker, I move that we recognize the Honorable Albert Garcia,

**[03:02:34.000] Rep. Arnan C. Panaligan** (asr-003120)  
Vice Chairperson of the Committee on Appropriations,

**[03:02:37.000] Rep. Arnan C. Panaligan** (asr-003121)  
to sponsor the proposed budget of the Department of Health.

**[03:02:40.000] Deputy Speaker Mercedes K. Alvarez** (asr-003122)  
Is there any objection? The Chair hears none.

**[03:02:43.000] Deputy Speaker Mercedes K. Alvarez** (asr-003123)  
Representative Senior Vice Chairperson Albert S. Garcia from the 2nd District of Bataan

**[03:02:48.000] Deputy Speaker Mercedes K. Alvarez** (asr-003124)  
is hereby recognized to begin the sponsorship of the proposed budget of the Department of Health,

**[03:02:54.000] Deputy Speaker Mercedes K. Alvarez** (asr-003125)  
including its attached agencies and corporations.

**[03:02:58.000] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-003126)  
Thank you very much Madam Speaker,

**[03:03:02.000] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-003127)  
Honorable Floor Leader,

**[03:03:05.000] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-003128)  
Magandang umaga po, distinguished colleagues.

**[03:03:08.000] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-003129)  
I have the honor to sponsor for consideration of this House,

**[03:03:13.000] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-003130)  
the proposed budget of the Department of Health and PhilHealth for Fiscal Year 2027

**[03:03:21.000] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-003131)  
in the amount of 263,624,

**[03:03:26.000] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-003132)  
289,000 in new appropriations.

**[03:03:30.000] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-003133)  
Together with automatic appropriations,

**[03:03:33.000] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-003134)  
the Department will administer a total program of 270.565 billion.

**[03:03:43.000] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-003135)  
The budget answers a clear constitutional command.

**[03:03:47.000] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-003136)  
Article 2, Section 15 and Article 13, Section 11

**[03:03:54.000] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-003137)  
of the Constitution direct the State to protect the right to health

**[03:04:00.000] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-003138)  
and to make essential health services available to all at all at affordable cost

**[03:04:07.000] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-003139)  
with priority for the poor.

**[03:04:10.000] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-003140)  
Republic Act No. 11223, the Universal Health Care Act,

**[03:04:17.000] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-003141)  
is the law that carries out that mandate.

**[03:04:20.000] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-003142)  
Every peso in this budget serves it.

**[03:04:24.000] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-003143)  
Of the new appropriations,

**[03:04:26.000] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-003144)  
110.367 billion funds personal services,

**[03:04:33.000] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-003145)  
134.232 billion funds maintenance and other operating expenses,

**[03:04:41.000] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-003146)  
and 19.026 billion funds capital outlays.

**[03:04:46.000] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-003147)  
The budget is built for the regions,

**[03:04:51.000] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-003148)  
161.886

**[03:04:54.000] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-003149)  
billion,

**[03:04:56.000] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-003150)  
or 61% is allocated directly to the 17 regions.

**[03:05:02.000] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-003151)  
The remaining 101.739 billion supports central operations.

**[03:05:10.000] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-003152)  
Five operating programs organize the work of the Department.

**[03:05:15.000] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-003153)  
Number 1, Health Facilities Operation Program,

**[03:05:19.000] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-003154)  
133.335 billion,

**[03:05:22.000] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-003155)  
the largest share,

**[03:05:24.000] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-003156)  
which sustains our public hospitals and health facilities.

**[03:05:29.000] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-003157)  
Number 2, Local Health System Support Program,

**[03:05:35.000] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-003158)  
69.613 billion.

**[03:05:40.000] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-003159)  
Number 3, Social Health Protection Program,

**[03:05:43.000] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-003160)  
27.989 billion.

**[03:05:47.000] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-003161)  
Number 4, Health Policy and Systems Strengthening Program,

**[03:05:52.000] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-003162)  
9.667 billion.

**[03:05:56.000] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-003163)  
Number 5, Health Regulatory Program,

**[03:05:59.000] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-003164)  
1.583 billion.

**[03:06:02.000] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-003165)  
Let me point to where these funds

**[03:06:05.000] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-003166)  
do the most direct good for our constituents.

**[03:06:10.000] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-003167)  
Hospital services,

**[03:06:12.000] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-003168)  
107.291 billion operates.

**[03:06:16.000] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-003169)  
The DOH, regional hospitals and other health facilities,

**[03:06:21.000] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-003170)  
and 29, I'm sorry, 28.189 billion operates the DOH hospitals in Metro Manila.

**[03:06:31.000] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-003171)  
This is the backbone of the public healthcare,

**[03:06:34.000] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-003172)  
the wards and emergency rooms our people depend on.

**[03:06:41.000] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-003173)  
For healthcare workforce,

**[03:06:44.000] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-003174)  
25.395 billion funds,

**[03:06:48.000] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-003175)  
the national health workforce,

**[03:06:50.000] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-003176)  
support system,

**[03:06:52.000] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-003177)  
which deploys doctors, nurses, midwives,

**[03:06:57.000] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-003178)  
and allied health professionals

**[03:06:59.000] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-003179)  
to the communities that need them most.

**[03:07:03.000] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-003180)  
For medical assistance,

**[03:07:05.000] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-003181)  
24.239 billion funds,

**[03:07:09.000] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-003182)  
medical assistance to indigent and financially incapacitated patients.

**[03:07:16.000] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-003183)  
This is the program behind the Malasakit Centers,

**[03:07:19.000] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-003184)  
it stands between a poor family

**[03:07:22.000] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-003185)  
and a financial ruin when illness strikes,

**[03:07:26.000] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-003186)  
and this is consistent with Republic Act No. 11463

**[03:07:31.000] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-003187)  
and Republic Act No. 8344.

**[03:07:36.000] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-003188)  
For health facilities,

**[03:15:07.740] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-003393)  
Sa first quarter ng 2027, idadagdag na po dito ang TB, HIV, STI, cervical cancer, and prostate cancer.

**[03:15:21.780] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-003394)  
By first quarter of 2028, dadagdag na po dito ang X-linked dystonia, glaucoma, and corneal transplant.

**[03:15:40.920] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-003395)  
Sa second quarter of this year, 2026, i-expand po sa maternity care.

**[03:15:51.780] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-003396)

**[03:16:06.200] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-003397)

**[03:31:25.460] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003780)

Mr. Speaker, distinguished sponsor,

**[03:31:33.560] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003782)

**[03:31:41.760] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003783)

**[03:31:51.900] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003784)

**[03:32:00.300] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-003785)

**[03:32:06.900] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-003786)  
Ang initiative po ngayon ng DOH regarding this ay,

**[03:32:11.760] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-003787)

**[03:32:18.320] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-003788)

**[03:32:25.540] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003789)  
Makikihingi na lang din po ng karampatang dokumento hinggil dito,

**[03:32:30.040] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003790)

**[03:32:37.900] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003791)  
Alinsunod po sa pahayag ni PBBM ukol sa long-term capacity,

**[03:32:41.760] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003792)

**[03:32:47.200] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003793)

**[03:32:59.820] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003795)

**[03:33:06.820] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003796)

**[03:33:10.360] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003797)

**[03:33:19.720] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003799)  
Mr. Speaker.

Mr. Speaker, distinguished colleague.

**[03:33:36.440] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-003801)  
So, ito po yung ginagawa ng DOH with regards to the FSL.

**[03:33:41.180] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-003802)  
Sa health facilities of the DOH and local government units, seek the guidance and assistance of the Komisyon sa Wikang Pilipino

**[03:33:51.300] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-003803)  
and or University of the Philippines for capacity building of the respective workforce on FSL in accordance with the law.

**[03:34:02.320] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-003804)  
At isa pa po yung ating mga health facilities ng DOH and LGUs in partnership with the relevant stakeholders and community groups,

**[03:34:11.180] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-003805)  
promote awareness and appreciation campaigns on FSL, including the observance of the International Day of Sign Languages every September 23.

**[03:34:25.440] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-003806)  
And we will also submit a report with regards to this, Mr. Speaker, distinguished colleague.

**[03:34:31.140] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003807)  
Thank you, Mr. Speaker. Maraming salamat po sa distinguished sponsor natin.

**[03:34:41.180] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003810)

**[03:34:53.700] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003811)

**[03:35:19.540] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-003815)

**[03:35:41.180] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-003816)  
So ito po para sa governance ng RA-11036 or mental health law, promotion and prevention ng MNS health literacy, peer-to-peer, school and workplace based mental health services, psychological first aid, MHPSS guidelines on ethical portrayal of

**[03:36:10.500] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-003817)  
suicide screening,

**[03:36:11.180] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-003818)  
MNS screening, risk of substance abuse, drug dependency examination, MH access program, community-based mental health and community-based drug rehab program, inpatient and outpatient benefit, sa 432 general mental health providers and 21 specialty mental health providers.

**[03:36:47.880] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-003819)  
Psychologists, 2,128; psychiatrists, 700; DevPed, 104; speech path, 1,225, but only 12 in health facilities.

**[03:37:15.220] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-003820)  
Sa occupational therapists, 3,401 but only 246 in healthcare facilities in the country, Mr. Speaker.

**[03:37:26.100] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003821)  
Makikihingi na lang din po ng datos na binanggit ko.

**[03:37:29.640] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-003822)  
We will do.

**[03:37:30.160] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003822-split-2)  
So Pinapatupad, Mr. Speaker, ng Department of Health yung World Health Organization's Mental Health Gap Action Program

**[03:37:38.580] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003823)  
para makapag-train ng primary care workers in non-specialized settings.

**[03:37:45.500] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003824)

**[03:37:55.640] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003825)  
Sa rugtong din po nito, ay maaari rin po bang mabigyan ang representasyong ito

**[03:38:01.640] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003826)

**[03:38:13.820] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-003827)

**[03:38:30.440] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-003829)

**[03:38:45.020] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003832)  
Thank you, Mr. Speaker, sa ating kaibigan na sponsor.

**[03:38:49.180] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003833)  
Sa huling set po ng mga katanungan natin ay papatungkol naman po ito sa Health Human Resource.

**[03:38:55.720] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003834)  
Sa usapin po ng kalusugan, walang serbisyo kung walang tao.

**[03:39:00.540] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003835)  
That is a basic fact.

**[03:39:01.620] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003836)  
Section 23 of the Universal Health Care Act mandates the DOH to formulate and implement

**[03:39:10.100] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003837)  
National Health Human Resource Master Plan to ensure the proper generation, recruitment, retooling,

**[03:43:01.620] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-003886)

**[03:43:14.460] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-003887)
