**[04:14:33.540] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-004736)  
sagot po sa tanong is

**[04:14:38.100] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-004737)  
33.3 million.

**[04:14:41.620] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004738)  
At magkano po yung nakukuha natin contributions

**[04:14:44.140] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004739)  
from our direct contributors,

**[04:14:46.460] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004740)  
Mr. Speaker, Distinguished Colleague?

**[04:14:50.940] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-004741)  
209 billion,

**[04:14:52.320] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-004742)  
Mr. Speaker, Honorable Colleague.

**[04:14:54.820] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004743)  
209 billion, no?

**[04:14:56.280] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004744)  
So, ilan po ang estimated

**[04:14:58.180] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004745)  
contributors natin na nakapagbabayad

**[04:15:00.000] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004746)  
consistently ng kanilang contributions.

**[04:15:02.200] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004747)  
Out of the 33 million members,

**[04:15:05.060] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004748)  
ilan po dyan yung,

**[04:15:06.000] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004749)  
ayan po yung ating mga direct contributors,

**[04:15:08.040] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004750)  
ilan po yung consistently nakakabayad sa kanila?

**[04:15:10.400] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004751)  
Kahit po prosyento lamang.

**[04:15:12.440] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-004752)  
Ang, in absolute numbers,

**[04:15:14.920] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-004753)  
ang nakakabayad po ng consistent for 12 months

**[04:15:18.040] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-004754)  
is 10.5 million, Mr. Speaker,

**[04:15:22.340] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-004755)  
Honorable Colleague.

**[04:15:23.140] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004756)  
So that's how many percent?

**[04:15:26.900] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-004757)  
That is around 31%.

**[04:15:29.140] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004758)  
31%.

**[04:15:30.620] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004759)  
So talaga po, not even half

**[04:15:32.940] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004760)  
yung nakakabayad consistently

**[04:15:34.800] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004761)  
ng kanilang premium.

**[04:15:37.540] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004762)  
Would Phil Health know

**[04:15:39.100] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004763)  
kung gano'ng karami ang active and registered contributors po

**[04:15:42.180] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004764)  
ng SSS at GSIS?

**[04:15:50.180] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-004765)  
They have a rough estimate, Mr. Speaker,

**[04:15:53.500] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-004766)  
Honorable Colleague.

**[04:15:54.980] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-004767)  
And according to them,

**[04:15:56.940] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-004768)  
it's around 18 million.

**[04:15:59.140] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004769)  
18 million for both SSS and GSIS?

**[04:16:07.260] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-004770)  
That's for SSS and they would need to double check

**[04:16:12.300] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-004771)  
with regards to the number of members from GSIS.

**[04:16:21.480] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004772)  
Pasensya na po, medyo mahalaga itong tanong na ito

**[04:16:24.300] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004773)  
doon sa point that this representation would like to drive at,

**[04:16:27.400] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004774)  
Mr. Speaker, Distinguished Sponsor,

**[04:16:29.140] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004775)  
again po, ilan po ang alam natin na registered

**[04:16:31.560] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004776)  
or active contributors ng SSS at GSIS?

**[04:16:35.940] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004777)  
Baka lang po mahugot natin mula sa Google.

**[04:16:41.180] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-004778)  
Mr. Speaker, Honorable Colleague,

**[04:16:43.060] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-004779)  
they are validating the numbers.

**[04:16:46.300] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-004780)  
Kung pwede pong mamaya po ibibigay po natin.

**[04:16:49.020] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004781)  
Sige po, Mr. Speaker, Distinguished Colleague,

**[04:16:52.500] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004782)  
sigurado po ako, mas malaki po yung kanila pong active contributors

**[04:16:57.260] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004783)  
kesa sa...

**[04:16:59.140] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004784)  
active contributors ng PhilHealth.

**[04:17:01.680] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004785)  
Inuulit ko po,

**[04:17:02.940] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004786)  
while waiting for the figures,

**[04:17:05.580] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004787)  
I can confidently say,

**[04:17:07.080] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004788)  
more than the active contributors of PhilHealth,

**[04:17:09.680] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004789)  
yung active contributors ng GSIS at saka po ng SSS.

**[04:17:14.140] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004790)  
And I won't be surprised kung meron pong asymmetry

**[04:17:17.160] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004791)  
kasi bakit mas maraming active contributors sa GSIS at saka sa SSS.

**[04:17:24.240] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004792)  
Which leads me to a very important point

**[04:17:26.600] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004793)  
which would later on be a proposal

**[04:17:28.340] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004794)  
to the PhilHealth and to the DOH.

**[04:17:31.160] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004795)  
Kung ganun po, sumasang-ayon ba ang PhilHealth

**[04:17:34.020] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004796)  
that we should institute reforms

**[04:17:36.180] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004797)  
in order to efficiently collect premiums?

**[04:17:39.300] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004798)  
Baka po, we can look into partnership with SSS,

**[04:17:43.160] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004799)  
GSIS, or in the long run, even with BIR.

**[04:17:46.860] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004800)  
Does PhilHealth have plans to strengthen its coordination

**[04:17:50.660] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004801)  
with other agencies to collect the premiums?

**[04:17:53.980] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004802)  
At sigurado po ako, hindi lamang ito magiging mas efficient,

**[04:17:57.340] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004803)  
makakabawas pa po.

**[04:17:58.340] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004804)  
Sa operating costs in terms of collection.

**[04:18:05.500] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-004805)  
The answer to that question, Mr. Speaker,

**[04:18:08.540] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-004806)  
Honorable Colleague, is yes.

**[04:18:11.260] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-004807)  
Tama po, to make the collection more efficient.

**[04:18:16.600] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-004808)  
Sa ngayon po, data sharing lang po ang nangyayari.

**[04:18:20.020] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-004809)  
Pero ang gustong mangyayari ng DOH and PhilHealth in the future

**[04:18:23.220] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-004810)  
is that these partner agencies

**[04:18:27.340] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-004811)  
or government corporation should collect the data

**[04:18:31.320] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-004812)  
at i-share po sa PhilHealth para mas maging efficient po yung collection.

**[04:18:37.840] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-004813)  
And that is what they are working on right now,

**[04:18:40.900] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-004814)  
Mr. Speaker, Honorable Colleague.

**[04:18:43.100] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004815)  
Mr. Speaker, Honorable Colleague,

**[04:18:44.660] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004816)  
are we also looking at the possibility of entering into partnership

**[04:18:49.140] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004817)  
with these corporations in terms of being collecting agents also of PhilHealth?

**[04:18:55.060] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-004818)  
The answer, Mr. Speaker, Honorable Colleague,

**[04:18:57.340] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-004819)  
is yes.

**[04:18:59.340] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-004820)  
Na through this formalization of partnership

**[04:19:03.520] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-004821)  
na sila na mag-collect on behalf of PhilHealth

**[04:19:06.960] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-004822)  
para mas maging efficient.

**[04:19:09.060] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004823)  
May timeline na po ba tayo dito?

**[04:19:10.680] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004824)  
Okay.

**[04:19:25.500] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-004825)  
Right now, Mr. Speaker,

**[04:19:27.080] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-004826)  
Mr. Speaker, Honorable Colleague,

**[04:19:28.360] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-004827)  
merong high-level discussion with the mentioned agencies

**[04:19:33.160] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-004828)  
at ang maganda po dito, kasama ang leadership ng DOF

**[04:19:38.600] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-004829)  
para talagang hopefully they would agree

**[04:19:42.400] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-004830)  
at mas maging efficient po yung collection in the future.

**[04:19:46.200] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004831)  
Sana po, Mr. Speaker, Distinguished Colleague,

**[04:19:48.840] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004832)  
mas maging mabilis yung pacing ng ating high-level discussions

**[04:19:52.600] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004833)  
which could eventually be translated into circular and necessary policies.

**[04:19:56.840] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004834)  
At kung mangangailangan po ng legislation,

**[04:19:59.160] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004835)  
we are very open to support the PhilHealth and the DOH

**[04:20:02.560] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004836)  
towards this direction.

**[04:20:04.020] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004837)  
Mahalaga po yung efficiency ng collection ng premium

**[04:20:06.840] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004838)  
kasi earlier na-illustrate ko po kung gaano kalayo

**[04:20:09.900] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004839)  
yung kailangan nating habulin to be able to fully cover

**[04:20:13.160] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004840)  
both direct and indirect contributors of the PhilHealth.

**[04:20:16.840] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004841)  
For the record lang po, Mr. Speaker, Distinguished Sponsor,

**[04:20:21.080] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004842)  
ano po yung mga improvements natin in terms of benefits

**[04:20:24.340] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004843)  
that are underway for 2027?

**[04:20:26.840] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004844)  
Ano po yung aabangan ng ating mga kababayan sa PhilHealth sa taong 2027?

**[04:20:40.840] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-004845)  
So, Mr. Speaker, Honorable Colleague, if I may enumerate one by one.

**[04:20:46.840] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-004846)  
So, una po yung tuberculosis inclusion of FLDs in gamot by January 1, 2027.

**[04:20:56.840] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-004847)  
NDOTS package enhanced and MDR package in 2027.

**[04:21:04.840] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-004848)  
HIV targeted publication of HOPE treats and HOPE prevents circular by December 1.

**[04:21:13.840] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-004849)  
Ang pangatlo po ay sa mga cancers naman, cervical, prostate, lung, liver, uterine, and ovarian

**[04:21:20.840] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-004850)  
under development for 2027.

**[04:21:26.840] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-004851)  
Family planning inclusion of contraceptives in gamot by January 1, 2027.

**[04:21:34.840] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-004852)  
Hepatitis under development po ito.

**[04:21:37.840] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-004853)  
Leprosy is also under development.

**[04:21:40.840] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-004854)  
Maternity and newborn care enhanced package issued last May.

**[04:21:51.840] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-004855)  
Ongoing po ang development work for inclusion of contraceptives.

**[04:21:54.840] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-004856)  
As postpartum package.

**[04:22:01.840] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-004857)  
Leukemia naman po under development sa CWDs or children with disabilities.

**[04:22:09.840] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-004858)  
Ito po ay mangyayari due for fourth quarter of this year.

**[04:22:24.840] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-004859)  
So, kasama po sa children with disability, ito pong Z package is also under review.

**[04:22:32.840] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-004860)  
Cardiac arrhythmia under study.

**[04:22:35.840] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-004861)  
Congenital heart disorder is also under study.

**[04:22:41.840] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-004862)  
So, these are some of the expanded packages na ginagawa po ng PhilHealth and [unclear].

**[04:22:46.840] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004863)  
Sa dental po, meron po ba?

**[04:22:48.840] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004864)  
Because oral care is also very important.

**[04:22:53.840] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004865)  
It's also very important, Mr. Speaker, Distinguished Sponsor.

**[04:23:02.840] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-004866)  
Meron po, Mr. Speaker, Honorable Colleagues, sa Q4 of 2027 naman po.

**[04:23:08.840] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-004867)  
So, Q4 of 2027, yung sa dental po natin.

**[04:23:11.840] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004868)  
Mr. Speaker, Distinguished Sponsor, it's very good to hear that there are a lot of reforms underway

**[04:23:17.840] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004869)  
under the new leadership of PhilHealth.

**[04:23:20.840] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004870)  
Gusto po nating maramdaman ng ordinaryong Pilipino,

**[04:23:22.840] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004871)  
na may PhilHealth tayo.

**[04:23:24.840] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004872)  
Gusto nating maramdaman ng mga direct contributors na sulit ang kanilang bawat pisong ibinabayad sa PhilHealth.

**[04:23:31.840] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004873)  
At masalong gusto nating maramdaman ng mga indirect contributors natin na may gobyernong handang sumalo at yumakap sa kanila.

**[04:23:39.840] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004874)  
Mr. Speaker, Good Sponsor,

**[04:23:40.840] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004875)  
Akbayan urges PhilHealth to explore ways to collect premiums more efficiently,

**[04:23:46.840] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004876)  
both from the private and public sector.

**[04:23:49.840] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004877)  
This representation, Mr. Speaker,

**[04:23:51.840] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004878)  
supports the increase of the budget of PhilHealth up to the amount that this government owes our indirect contributors.

**[04:23:59.840] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004879)  
Tapatan naman natin ang binabayad ng direct contributors through premium subsidies.

**[04:24:04.840] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004880)  
After all, this is the essence of solidarity.

**[04:24:08.840] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004881)  
This is the essence of universal healthcare.

**[04:24:11.840] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004882)  
Mr. Speaker, Distinguished Sponsor, I'd like to move on to another issue,

**[04:24:15.840] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004883)  
and that is the issue of injectable PrEP. Ito po yung pre-exposure prophylaxis natin.

**[04:24:21.840] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004884)  
Lenacapavir is a long-acting injectable form of PrEP given twice a year.

**[04:24:27.840] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004885)  
In July 2025, WHO recommended it as an additional PrEP option.

**[04:24:33.840] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004886)  
In fact, the US FDA approved it in June 2025.

**[04:24:39.840] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004887)  
In February 2026, the Department of Health announced publicly that injectable PrEP was coming to the Philippines through PEPFAR.

**[04:24:48.840] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004888)  
Pero magpipitong buwan na po.

**[04:24:50.840] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004889)  
Wala pa ring acceptance decision, registration issuance at rollout date for Lenacapavir.

**[04:24:57.840] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004890)  
Mr. Speaker, good sponsor, does the Health Secretary consider Lenacapavir a critically needed drug under AO 2020-001?

**[04:25:12.840] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-004891)  
The answer, Mr. Speaker, honorable colleague, is yes.

**[04:25:20.840] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-004892)  
It's already recognized by the DOH under Administrative Order No. 2026-0005.

**[04:25:31.840] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004893)  
And would the good Secretary consider Lenacapavir a game-changer sa ating HIV response?

**[04:25:38.840] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004894)  
Will it save lives?

**[04:25:42.840] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-004895)  
The answer is yes, Mr. Speaker.

**[04:25:48.840] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-004896)  
That is one of the options.

**[04:25:50.840] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-004897)  
And it is still being validated as we speak, Mr. Speaker, honorable colleague.

**[04:25:58.840] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004898)  
Mr. Speaker, honorable colleague, the most important question in this series is, will the Secretary sign the determination?

**[04:26:06.840] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004899)  
Can the good Secretary give us a timeline for the signing of this determination?

**[04:26:30.860] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-004902)  
Right now, Mr. Speaker, honorable colleague, kina-fast-track po ito ng ating Kalihim ng DOH with the FDA.

**[04:26:40.840] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-004904)  
At kapag lumabas na po yung Certificate of Product Registration, ay dun po.

**[04:26:56.040] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-004905)  
Pwede na pong gamitin ito specific to that cohort.

**[04:26:59.720] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004906)  
Thank you very much, Mr. Speaker, Distinguished Colleague. Medyo kinabahan po ako na marinig ko yung FDA.

**[04:27:04.340] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004907)  
Kasi nung po ating budget briefing, nakita naman natin kung gano'ng kalaki at kadami ang backlog ng FDA at kung gano'ng kabagal yung proseso dahil nga po sa kakulangan ng tao.

**[04:27:14.380] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004908)  
So nung bilanggit nyo yung FDA sa conversation, natakot ako na baka mas talong bumagal pa.

**[04:27:20.240] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004909)  
Inuulit ko lang po, this is WHO recommended at pwede rin pong US FDA approval since June 2025.

**[04:27:27.000] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004910)  
Pag sinabi pong fast track, gano'ng po kabilis yung fast track, Mr. Speaker, Distinguished Colleague?

**[04:27:34.340] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-004911)  
Mr. Speaker, Honorable Colleague, meron naman pong commitment with the leadership of FDA para i-fast track po ito.

**[04:27:42.340] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004912)

**[04:29:05.120] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004926)

**[04:29:21.960] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004930)  
Hindi ko na po tatanungin kung ilan yung unagi HIV positive every day kasi alam naman po natin na report naman yan po ulit-ulit.

**[04:29:28.320] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004931)

**[04:29:31.060] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004932)

**[04:29:33.660] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004933)

**[04:29:36.800] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004934)

**[04:29:45.060] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004935)  
In the past 7 months, na-delayed po ang rollout ng Lenacapavir.

**[04:29:51.180] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004936)

**[04:29:54.980] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004937)

**[04:30:00.000] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004938)

**[04:30:06.200] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004939)

**[04:30:14.620] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004940)

**[04:30:17.280] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004941)

**[04:30:23.680] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004942)

**[04:30:27.140] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004943)  
One of the highest, if not the highest, in the region.

**[04:30:29.340] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004944)  
And this is a game changer.

**[04:30:32.840] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004945)

**[04:30:39.560] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004946)

**[04:30:43.120] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004947)

**[04:30:47.540] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004948)

**[04:30:51.620] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004949)

**[04:30:58.280] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004950)

**[04:30:59.340] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004951)

**[04:31:00.340] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004952)

**[04:31:07.340] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004954)

**[04:31:13.340] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004955)

**[04:31:18.340] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004956)  
May nabanggit na po kayo kanina, additional coverage.

**[04:31:21.340] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004957)  
Noong committee hearing po, nagkaroon tayo ng pagkakataon na mga isa publiko ang nakababahalang bilang ng mga kababaihang Pilipinas.

**[04:31:30.340] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004960)  
na nakakaranas ng rapid repeat pregnancies o ang sunod-sunod na pagkabuntis sa loob lamang ng 24 buwan or 2 years

**[04:31:39.340] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004961)  
Ito po ay may negative health outcomes, hindi lamang sa nanay, kundi at pati sa baby, kasama na ang risk ng maternal at infant death.

**[04:31:49.320] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004963)  
Nais po nating malaman mula sa DOH

**[04:31:50.320] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004964)

**[04:31:59.340] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-004966)

**[04:32:13.660] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-004967)

**[04:32:25.300] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004968)

**[04:32:29.500] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004969)  
Mr. Speaker, Distinguished Colleague.

**[04:32:31.260] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-004970)  
Three months po, Mr. Speaker.

**[04:32:33.660] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004971)

**[04:32:36.160] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004972)

**[04:32:47.880] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004974)

**[04:32:59.340] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-004976)

**[04:33:14.870] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-004978)

**[04:33:27.460] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004979)

**[04:33:52.380] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004982)

**[04:33:57.480] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004983)

**[04:33:59.340] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004984)

**[04:34:07.120] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004985)

**[04:34:31.940] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-004995)

**[04:35:38.300] Rep. Ivan Howard A. Guintu** (asr-005008)  
Mr. Speaker, I move that we recognize the Honorable Bong Suntay of the 4th District of Quezon City to interpolate the proposed budget of the DOH.

**[04:35:49.160] Senior Deputy Speaker Ferdinand L. Hernandez** (asr-005009)  
Honorable Bong Suntay is hereby recognized.

**[04:35:52.240] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005010)  
Thank you very much, Mr. Speaker.

**[04:35:55.800] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005011)  
To our good sponsor, of course, to the

**[04:35:59.080] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005012)  
Department of Health family, magandang tanghali po sa inyong lahat.

**[04:36:03.060] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005013)  
Magandang hapon.

**[04:36:08.040] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005014)  
The Universal Healthcare Act, RA 11223, explicitly defines indirect contributors as Filipinos,

**[04:36:16.220] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005015)  
not categorized as direct contributors, including indigent, senior citizens, persons with disabilities,

**[04:36:25.080] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005016)  
whose health insurance premiums shall be subsidized

**[04:36:29.080] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005017)  
by the national government.

**[04:36:30.300] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005018)  
While the Universal Healthcare Act does not explicitly fix an exact peso amount for the premium of indirect contributors,

**[04:36:38.640] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005019)  
the provisions on government premium subsidies is within the exact same provision as the premiums of direct contributors under Section 10

**[04:36:46.520] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005020)  
and must be construed harmoniously.

**[04:36:52.460] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005021)  
Historically and prior to the UHC Act,

**[04:36:56.580] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005022)  
the floor rate of direct contributors served...

**[04:36:59.080] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005023)  
as the benchmark for subsidized member.

**[04:37:02.480] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005024)  
But under the 2027 NEP,

**[04:37:06.080] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005025)  
the 55.42 billion allocations for 30.74 million indirect contributors amounts to a meager 1,803 per beneficiary per year,

**[04:37:17.180] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005026)  
far below the premium for the lowest income floor.

**[04:37:21.720] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005027)  
So my question is, who sets the premium rate for indirect contributors and what legal and actual basis was used?

**[04:37:29.080] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005028)  
In the 2027 NEP, to justify subsidizing vulnerable Filipinos at only 1,803 per year?

**[04:37:50.180] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-005029)  
Mr. Speaker, Honorable Colleague,

**[04:37:53.960] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-005030)  
ito po yung katanungan na sinagot din po kanina,

**[04:37:58.340] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-005031)  
yung discrepancy...

**[04:37:59.080] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-005032)  
nung figures ng DBM at ng PhilHealth.

**[04:38:04.120] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-005033)  
So, di hamak na mas maliit yung figures ng DBM,

**[04:38:08.440] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-005034)  
kaya pag dinivide nyo, talaga ang liit ng package.

**[04:38:10.700] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-005035)  
So, ang ginagawa po ng PhilHealth,

**[04:38:13.780] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-005036)  
basta kung ano yung halaga ng package na ibibigay sa member,

**[04:38:20.880] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-005037)  
yun po yung kinukover ng PhilHealth.

**[04:38:24.220] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005038)  
Hindi ko tinatanong yung coverage.

**[04:38:26.100] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005039)  
Ang tinatanong ko yung contribution,

**[04:38:29.080] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005040)  
yung contributors.

**[04:38:30.000] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005041)  
Kasi nakalagay sa NEP, as I mentioned,

**[04:38:33.880] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005042)  
it's 55.42 billion ang na-allocate under sa NEP

**[04:38:38.400] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005043)  
for 30.74 indirect beneficiaries,

**[04:38:42.240] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005044)  
yung mga hindi nagbabayad ng contribution.

**[04:38:44.600] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005045)  
So, ibig sabihin, ito yung subsidized ng ating pamahalaan.

**[04:38:49.480] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005046)  
If you divide it by 30.74,

**[04:38:52.680] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005047)  
1,803 lang yan.

**[04:38:54.380] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005048)  
That's far below yung dating ginagamit before the approval of...

**[04:38:58.920] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005049)  
of UHC.

**[04:39:17.880] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-005051)  
Ang position po dito, Mr. Speaker,

**[04:39:21.200] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-005052)  
Honorable Colleague ng PhilHealth,

**[04:39:23.720] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-005053)  
ay

**[04:39:23.880] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-005054)  
imbis na headcount

**[04:39:27.680] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-005055)  
ang naging...

**[04:39:28.920] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-005056)  
naging basis ng DBM maging budget-based na lang po

**[04:39:32.960] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-005057)  
ang sinasabi ng ating PhilHealth

**[04:39:36.420] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-005058)  
for ease of planning and resource allocation.

**[04:39:40.960] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-005059)  
So, talagang meron pong deficit

**[04:39:43.880] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-005060)  
or pagkukulang sa pondong ibinigay sa PhilHealth.

**[04:39:48.800] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005061)  
So, well, naano lang ako eh.

**[04:39:51.820] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005062)  
Aside from yung mababa akong base contribution,

**[04:39:55.240] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005063)  
kung ngayon sasabihin...

**[04:39:56.360] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005064)  
Kasi ang worry ko dyan,

**[04:39:57.960] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005065)  
given that yung senior citizens,

**[04:40:01.300] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005066)  
persons with disability,

**[04:40:02.660] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005067)  
yung mga indigent family

**[04:40:04.300] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005068)  
inherently face

**[04:40:05.640] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005069)  
a higher health risk

**[04:40:09.180] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005070)  
and medical expenditure

**[04:40:10.780] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005071)  
than relatively healthier

**[04:40:12.440] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005072)  
formally employed workers,

**[04:40:15.740] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005073)  
parang masado ngayon maliit yung baseline.

**[04:40:18.640] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005074)  
Now, who will choose?

**[04:40:21.360] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005075)  
Kung sasabihin natin as per

**[04:40:23.540] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005076)  
beneficiary base,

**[04:40:26.340] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005077)  
kung sino yung unang makakalapit,

**[04:40:27.960] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005078)  
kung sino yung unang makakalapit,

**[04:40:28.960] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005079)  
unahan ba yan?

**[04:40:29.960] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005080)  
Kung sino yung unang makakalapit?

**[04:40:30.960] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005081)  
Who chooses?

**[04:40:31.960] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005082)

**[04:40:32.960] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005083)

**[04:40:35.960] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-005084)  
Mr. Speaker, honorable colleague,

**[04:40:38.960] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-005085)  
Siyempre, hindi po pwedeng tanggalin ng PhilHealth

**[04:40:41.960] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-005086)

**[04:40:46.960] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-005087)

**[04:56:01.140] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-005531)

**[04:57:32.540] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-005558)

**[05:01:50.060] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005640)

**[05:01:53.420] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005641)

**[05:01:55.360] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005642)

**[05:02:04.280] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-005644)

**[05:03:02.140] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005657)

**[05:04:14.520] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005675)

**[05:04:48.380] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005683)

**[05:04:54.780] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005684)

**[05:04:56.940] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-005685)

**[05:12:01.720] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005847)  
Thank you, Mr. Speaker, thank you, majority leader.

**[05:12:05.720] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005848)  
Mayong hapon sa

**[05:12:09.700] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005849)  
kagalang-galang na sponsor at sa ating Secretary ng Department of Health at sa buong DOH family.

**[05:12:19.180] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005850)  
Paikse lang po ang aking interpellation. Related pa din po sa power yung isa.

**[05:12:28.780] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005851)  
Would the distinguished sponsor yield to a few clarifications?

**[05:12:35.140] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-005852)  
Certainly, Mr. Speaker, lalong-lalo na mula sa ating most respected colleague, kay Congressman Dago.

**[05:12:44.300] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005853)  
Thank you, Mr. Speaker. Thank you, distinguished sponsor.

**[05:12:48.720] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005854)  
Doon po sa mga project natin sa HPIP, but this does not only happen in the Department of Health.

**[05:13:01.920] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005855)  
Pareho-pareho po sa DepEd,

**[05:13:05.140] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005856)  
sa CAAP, na medyo nagsasayang tayo ng pera.

**[05:13:12.340] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005857)  
For example, yung mga super health center natin, ang lalaki ng mga transformer, nilakihan yung sino yung ano, anong unit ng DOH yung gumagawa ng plano, napakalaki, 50 kVA.

**[05:13:33.020] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005858)  
Now, anong implication nito?

**[05:13:35.140] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005859)  
Doon pa lang sa cost ng transformer, malaki na.

**[05:13:39.800] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005860)  
Pag naikabit yan, yung health center, for example, wala namang laman.

**[05:13:51.120] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005861)  
Wala nga ng nurse eh.

**[05:13:53.040] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005862)  
Ang estimate doon sa 50 kVA ay may X-ray, may kung anu-anong medical equipment.

**[05:14:00.820] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005863)  
Pero, ang aktual, wala.

**[05:14:05.140] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005864)  
At kaya, resulta, nagbabayad tayo sa cost ng transformer, nagbabayad tayo sa minimum charge ng transformer na yan, kasi may kontrata yan sa distribution utility.

**[05:14:18.200] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005865)  
Whether na gamit mo yung transformer o hindi, binabayaran yun.

**[05:14:22.900] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005866)  
Then, ang worst, napuputulan.

**[05:14:25.760] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005867)  
Dahil sa laki ng bayarin, hindi kakayanin ng provincial health office.

**[05:14:33.260] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005868)  
Kasi ito, turnover sa...

**[05:14:35.140] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005869)  
LGU yan.

**[05:14:36.440] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005870)  
LGU managed.

**[05:14:37.420] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005871)  
So, that's one.

**[05:14:40.000] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005872)  
Na, distinguished sponsor, pwedeng tingnan yung mga nationally funded projects na ito na tine-turn over natin sa LGU,

**[05:14:51.300] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005873)  
dapat tamang-tama lang yung mga transformer na ilagay.

**[05:14:55.640] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005874)  
Kung maaari nga, i-connect na lang muna natin sa...

**[05:15:00.000] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005875)  
Sa common transformer, walang babayaran na minimum.

**[05:15:03.800] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005876)  
Later on, kung may mabili na tayo na gamit, isa ka lang magpakontrata.

**[05:15:09.200] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-005877)  
Thank you, Mr. Speaker, Honorable Colleague.

**[05:15:14.160] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-005878)  
Nakita rin po ng ating bagong kalihim ng health, itong problema nito.

**[05:15:19.960] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-005879)  
And tama po kayo, yung karamihan po dito ay dapat responsibilidad ng ating local government units.

**[05:15:25.300] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-005880)  
Pero siguro dahil sa kakulangan din ng pondo, hindi napopondohan yung mga pinirmahan nila under the MOA.

**[05:15:35.160] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-005881)  
Kaya ngayon, meron ng prerequisites na gagawin ang Department of Health

**[05:15:40.880] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-005882)  
para simula pa lang ay mag-garantee na po yung responsibilidad ng ating mga local government units under this program.

**[05:15:52.460] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-005883)  
And to add to that,

**[05:15:54.760] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-005884)  
magtatayo na po ng bagong unit under the DOH.

**[05:15:59.000] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-005885)  
Ito po ang tinatawag na hospital cluster.

**[05:16:05.020] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-005886)  
At nasa ilalim po nito, itong Health Facility Development Bureau.

**[05:16:10.320] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-005887)  
Sila po yung mga nagde-design.

**[05:16:12.440] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-005888)  
Tapos, lalagyan pa, ito bagong innovation po ito,

**[05:16:16.100] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-005889)  
lalagyan pa na ni Secretary Mercado ng project manager

**[05:16:20.500] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-005890)  
sa central office that will oversee the

**[05:16:24.760] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-005891)  
the CHDs at yung mga ini-implement na mga HFEP sa local government units.

**[05:16:31.440] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-005892)  
Nang sa ganon, talagang matutukan kung ano lang yung dapat at walang maging wastage and inefficiencies

**[05:16:39.000] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-005893)  
na nangyayari sa implementation na ito.

**[05:16:43.000] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-005894)  
Dahil napaka-kakaunti na lang po ng ating pondo ang hirap akong maraming inefficiencies and wastage.

**[05:16:52.280] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005895)  
I think, Distinguished Sponsor,

**[05:16:54.600] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005896)  
maganda po yung napag-isipang gagawin ng ating bagong Health Secretary.

**[05:17:01.500] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005897)  
Pero idagdag nyo na lang, bago isama yung project na yan,

**[05:17:07.180] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005898)  
mag-due diligence muna kung yung paglalagyan, may nurse ba,

**[05:17:11.160] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005899)  
baka building nandun, pero ni isang nurse wala.

**[05:17:15.660] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005900)  
So, nandun yung halimbawa medical equipment,

**[05:17:19.520] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005901)  
eh walang gumagamit dahil alam naman natin yung kakulangan,

**[05:17:24.600] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005902)  
nandun naman natin ng doktor at saka nurse, yung anak ko mismo, nurse eh.

**[05:17:28.600] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005903)  
So, kaya alam ko yung mga yan.

**[05:17:30.600] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005904)  
Kung minsan ako ang nagbabayad para hindi maputulan,

**[05:17:33.600] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005905)  
kasi kakaunti ang gamit, malaki yung binabayaran dahil may minimum nga,

**[05:17:38.600] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005906)  
dahil may transformer na sa linya.

**[05:17:41.600] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005907)  
So, yan po, yun yung reality.

**[05:17:43.600] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005908)  
Now, pangalawa po, mayroong kuha report regarding dito sa mga medical equipment,

**[05:17:54.600] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005909)  
na hindi na-deliver, kung tama itong data ko,

**[05:17:58.600] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005910)  
mga 338 contracts ito na worth 242 million.

**[05:18:03.600] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005911)  
Tama po ba ito, Distinguished Sponsor?

**[05:18:05.600] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005912)  
Mr. Speaker?

**[05:18:10.600] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-005913)  
May we clarify, Mr. Speaker, honorable colleague, kung anong taon po,

**[05:18:15.600] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-005914)  
para lang ma-check kaagad yung COA report?

**[05:18:19.600] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-005915)  
Kung meron pong taon?

**[05:18:22.600] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005916)  
Nakuha lang po namin ito.

**[05:18:25.600] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005917)  
Ang isa pa, if this is correct, yung mga project natin sa HPIP na may delay o hindi natapos,

**[05:18:38.600] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005918)  
488 HPIP infra projects amounting to 17.2 billion.

**[05:18:46.600] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005919)  
Please correct me if I'm wrong, kung tama yan.

**[05:18:51.600] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005920)  
Kasi, for example, in Siargao, mayroon akong isa dyan na nakita sa Socorro na hindi nga natapos.

**[05:19:01.600] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005921)  
Kaya, dalawang beses na sumulat si Mayor na tulungan ko na lang yung linya para ma-energize yung ano,

**[05:19:09.600] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005922)  
kasi mag-i-inspect na daw yung COA, yung mga ganon.

**[05:19:12.600] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005923)  
So, kung minsan, I must be candid na sabihin ito,

**[05:19:19.600] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005924)  
para malaman ni sekretary.

**[05:19:22.600] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005925)  
Kung minsan, sinasabi ko, huwag na munang i-hang up, i-connect yung transformer para walang babayaran.

**[05:19:29.600] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005926)  
Eh, sasabihin ng DOH or ng contractor, ay kailangan GM,

**[05:19:37.600] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005927)  
kailangan para pag-inspect ng COA, tama sa program of work na 50 kVA.

**[05:19:42.600] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005928)  
Kaya, ang sabi ko na lang, sige, after na ano, para hindi tayo magkapurisyo dyan,

**[05:19:47.600] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005929)  
kasi kung minsan, sa akin din lumalapit na huwag putulan.

**[05:19:50.600] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005930)  
Sabi ko, after ano, i-disconnect lang natin, ilipat ko kayo doon sa walang transformer.

**[05:19:55.600] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005931)  
Basta lang na-inspect na ng COA.

**[05:19:57.600] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005932)  
So, I'm not saying na there are anomalous, pero medyo mali yung ginagawa natin.

**[05:20:04.600] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005933)  
Yung pundo na very meager, yung hirap na nga yung kongreso pag-allocate kung saan,

**[05:20:11.600] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005934)  
because of the limited fiscal space.

**[05:20:14.600] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005935)  
Eh, kung dinisign na lang siya.

**[05:20:17.600] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005936)  
Sana yun.

**[05:20:18.600] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005937)  
In relation sa issue ko kanina.

**[05:20:20.600] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005938)  
So, pakicheck lang, distinguished sponsor, Secretary, kung tama yung nabasa ko na mga COA report.

**[05:20:34.600] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005939)  
Yun lang naman po ang mga issue na yung kailangan para sa akin, kailangan i-address,

**[05:20:44.600] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005940)  
kasi nagsasayang tayo ng pundo.

**[05:20:46.600] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005941)  
Magamit pa sana yun sa ibang mga programa natin.

**[05:20:53.600] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005942)  
Thank you very much.

**[05:20:54.600] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-005943)  
We agree and as mentioned earlier, yung sistema natin dito sa pag-design,

**[05:20:59.600] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-005944)  
paglagay ng bagong project manager,

**[05:21:02.600] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-005945)  
pagtayo ng bagong unit, itong hospital cluster that will address this.

**[05:21:10.600] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-005946)  
And the DOH, the Secretary, took note of your comments.

**[05:21:16.600] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-005947)  
And the problem in that hospital.

**[05:21:19.600] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-005948)  
If we may receive more details para mapuntahan po ng ating CHD at maayos po yung mga problema na ito.

**[05:21:28.600] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005949)  
Sige po, Distinguished Sponsor.

**[05:21:30.600] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005950)  
Pero ang tinutukoy ko po dito, parang tip of the iceberg yung mga yan,

**[05:21:36.600] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005951)  
kagaya ng classroom sa DepEd, na this is happening nationwide.

**[05:21:41.600] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005952)  
Na mayroon tayong mga building na hindi naman nagagamit.

**[05:21:44.600] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005953)  
Ginawa lang bodega.

**[05:21:46.600] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005954)  
Ginawa lang bodega.

**[05:21:47.600] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005955)  
Ginamit ng ibang klaseng gamit dahil kulang nga tayo ng nurse.

**[05:21:54.600] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005956)  
Mayroon na silang health center.

**[05:21:56.600] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005957)  
Dadagdagan pa natin ng super

**[05:21:58.600] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005958)  
ang tawag natin, di ba tama? Super Health Center.

**[05:22:02.600] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005959)  
Paano naging super? Hindi ko alam bakit naging super yun.

**[05:22:06.600] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005960)  
Eh wala namang mai-assign doon ng mga medical staff.

**[05:22:12.600] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005961)  
So yung mga program na yun, ang titingnan natin, if those buildings are necessary ba talaga?

**[05:22:21.600] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005962)  
Kung sa tingin ko necessary, hindi siguro sa lahat ng lugar, medyo mga piling lugar,

**[05:22:28.600] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005963)  
kagaya ng Siargao, hindi lahat ng Siargao kailangan ng super health center.

**[05:22:35.600] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005964)  
General law na maybe, kasi nandun yung mga turista.

**[05:22:39.600] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005965)  
So yan yung sabi ko kanina.

**[05:22:41.600] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005966)  
Na due diligence muna bago isama sa project listing yung lugar na yan.

**[05:22:48.600] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005967)  
Din yung appropriate lang na halimbawa sa power, yung appropriate na laki ng building,

**[05:22:56.600] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005968)  
dipindi sa pangangailangan doon.

**[05:22:59.600] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005969)  
Kasi kahit gaano kalaki yun, eh wala namang pasinti doon dahil wala ngang doktor at saka ano.

**[05:23:06.600] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005970)  
Sayang yung building natin.

**[05:23:08.600] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005971)  
Thank you, Distinguished Sponsor.

**[05:23:10.600] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005972)  
Thank you, Sekretary.

**[05:23:11.600] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-005973)  
Thank you, Mr. Speaker.

**[05:23:12.600] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-005974)  
Thank you very much, Mr. Speaker, and to the Honorable Dagooc. Maraming salamat po.

Majority Leader.

**[05:23:19.600] Rep. Ernesto "Ernix" Dionisio Jr.** (asr-005976)  
Mr. Speaker, next to interpellate the sponsor, I move to recognize Representative Presley C. De Jesus of PHILRECA Partylist.

**[05:23:28.600] Senior Deputy Speaker Ferdinand L. Hernandez** (asr-005977)  
Honorable Presley De Jesus is hereby recognized.

**[05:23:33.600] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-005978)  
Thank you.

**[05:23:35.600] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-005980)  
Senior Deputy Speaker, Mr. Speaker, and of course, to my good friend,

**[05:23:41.600] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-005984)  
the distinguished sponsor.

**[05:23:43.600] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-005985)  
Most of the questions already been asked.

**[05:23:45.600] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-005986)  
So ako siguro manifestation at may konting question lang.

**[05:23:50.600] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-005987)  
Ito siguro, I don't know if it's been asked already.

**[05:23:53.600] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-005988)  
How about yung mga hotlines natin?

**[05:23:58.600] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-005989)  
Video calls and mobile apps for medical advice.

**[05:24:01.600] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-005990)  
Meron ba tayo ganyan? Kasi some of them may ganyan sila eh.

**[05:24:04.600] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-005991)  
Lalo kung hating gabi, malayo.

**[05:24:08.600] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-005992)  
Nandiyan na ba tayo? Are we on that stage?

**[05:24:23.600] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-005993)  
Meron po, Mr. Speaker, honorable colleague.

**[05:24:27.600] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-005994)  
Ito po yung 24/7 NCMH crisis hotline, which is, if I may announce,

**[05:24:36.600] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-005995)  
1553.

**[05:24:42.600] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-005996)  
And it has handled 129,488 calls thus far.

**[05:24:50.600] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-005997)  
And this has been established since May of 2019.

**[05:24:58.600] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-005998)  
Thank you, Mr. Speaker, of course, Distinguished Sponsor.

**[05:25:01.600] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-005999)  
Kasama rin ho ba rito yung mga private natin mga hospitals?

**[05:25:06.600] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-006000)  
O eto, more on ano pa lang.

**[05:25:08.600] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-006001)  
So do we have a memorandum agreement sa mga private

**[05:25:11.600] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-006002)  
or more on ano pa lang yung mga government?

**[05:25:18.600] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-006003)  
So this hotline is run by DOH, so government.

**[05:25:22.600] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-006004)  
Pero kahit sino po sa ating mga kababayan ay pwedeng tumawag po 24-7.

**[05:25:28.600] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-006005)  
Thank you, Mr. Speaker, Distinguished Sponsor.

**[05:25:31.600] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-006006)  
As I point out, kasi ilang bes na akong, siyempre,

**[05:25:35.600] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-006007)  
we have the privilege na namimigay tayo ng MAIP.

**[05:25:39.600] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-006008)  
But for me, MAIP is the best, no?

**[05:25:41.600] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-006009)  
Thanks to DOH family na ito talaga, napaka-importante ito.

**[05:25:46.600] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-006010)  
Ang daming natutulungan na ito.

**[05:25:48.600] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-006011)  
But may kasamang problema.

**[05:25:51.600] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-006012)  
Not the DOH itself, no?

**[05:25:54.600] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-006013)  
Paano natin, how can we do to help our kababayan?

**[05:26:00.600] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-006014)  
Yung pagbayad. Kasi some of the doctors, they don't accept yung ano eh.

**[05:26:04.600] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-006015)  
Hanggang ngayon eh, ilan na yun na.

**[05:26:06.600] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-006016)  
Misan kami na nag-abono.

**[05:26:08.600] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-006017)  
Kawawang ko. Especially indigent.

**[05:26:10.600] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-006018)  
Can we have some, like, category?

**[05:26:13.600] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-006019)  
Pag talagang indigent, sana ano na, kahit sino ang doktor na.

**[05:26:17.600] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-006020)  
Although, private yan, hindi natin maano.

**[05:26:20.600] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-006021)  
Government, we have no problem.

**[05:26:22.600] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-006022)  
Pero pagdating sa private, Mr. Speaker, Distinguished Sponsor.

**[05:26:28.600] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-006023)  
Thank you, Mr. Speaker, Honorable Colleague, for that question.

**[05:26:32.600] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-006024)  
Talagang problema po yan nationwide.

**[05:26:34.600] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-006025)  
At nararamdaman po natin as representatives of our people.

**[05:26:40.600] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-006026)  
Kaya ito po palaging sinasabi ng ating Secretary of Health na with regards to the private hospitals,

**[05:26:48.600] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-006027)  
dahil wala namang problema sa government hospitals,

**[05:26:52.600] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-006028)  
pagdating sa hospital bill at professional fee,

**[05:26:57.600] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-006029)  
isasama na po sa bagong MOA.

**[05:27:00.600] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-006030)  
Ang mga guidelines na ito para in-check po yung ibibill na hospital bill at professional fee.

**[05:27:11.600] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-006031)  
So, kapag sila po'y pumirma dito sa bagong MOA,

**[05:27:15.600] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-006032)  
merong opt-in condition na ibig sabihin sila ay magiging bahagi ng bagong sistema.

**[05:27:25.600] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-006033)  
So, pati ho yung rates.

**[05:27:29.600] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-006034)  
Ay ilalagay na sa tamang level.

**[05:27:32.600] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-006035)  
Makakap na po ito.

**[05:27:34.600] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-006036)  
At yung dating tinatanggihan ng mga private doctors na professional fee coming from the MAIP,

**[05:27:43.600] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-006037)  
kung may opt-in po sila, ibig sabihin kasama po sila doon sa usapan.

**[05:27:48.600] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-006038)  
Plus, yung binanggit ko po kanina na malaking tulong po ito,

**[05:27:52.600] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-006039)  
yung 10% of the bed capacity ng private hospital,

**[05:27:58.600] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-006040)  
kailangan ma-establish as ward,

**[05:28:01.600] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-006041)  
nang sa ganon talagang ma-increase yung bed capacity na pwedeng puntahan ng ating mga kababayan nationwide.

**[05:28:10.600] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-006042)  
Kung saan man merong private hospital kasama ng mga government hospital sa lugar na yun.

**[05:28:18.600] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-006043)  
So, these are some of the innovations na talagang tututukan ng ating Secretary of Health and President and CEO of PhilHealth.

**[05:28:27.600] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-006044)  
We know that PhilHealth and DOH intertwine yan eh.

**[05:28:32.600] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-006045)  
So, kailangan talaga they work together.

**[05:28:35.600] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-006046)  
Ako, matupad lang yan.

**[05:28:37.600] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-006047)  
Talagang siguro, ginhawa na sa atin lahat ito.

**[05:28:40.600] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-006048)  
Lalo na yung mga kababayan natin.

**[05:28:42.600] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-006049)  
Talagang yan ang problema nila noon naiwalay.

**[05:28:45.600] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-006050)  
Tapos, do you think, can we do it before the end of this year?

**[05:28:50.600] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-006051)  
O nagsisimula na ba yan?

**[05:28:52.600] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-006052)

**[05:29:15.600] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-006057)

**[06:27:22.540] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-007465)  
Tungkol naman po ito sa PhilHealth contributions ng ating overseas Filipino workers.

**[06:27:28.900] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-007466)  
Pansin po natin na may dating circular na ang PhilHealth which directs overseas Filipino workers including land-based and sea-based workers to pay 3% of their income as a mandatory premium contribution with the premium rate increasing annually by 0.5% until 2024.

**[06:27:46.900] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-007467)  
At hindi po nakakapagpanatagloob sa ating mga OFWs na makita yung corruption issue na hinarap ng PhilHealth several years back.

**[06:28:01.200] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-007468)  
And with the current economic situation and the unresolved PhilHealth corruption issues, mas napopronounce yung kahirapan ng mga mamayang Pilipino sa contributions na lumalaki even up to 2024.

**[06:28:14.540] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-007469)  
At hindi po makapagpanatagloob.

**[06:28:16.900] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-007470)  
Tugma at maaaring may pagkaiba-iba sa mga sitwasyon depende po kung saan naka-estasyon ang mga OFWs.

**[06:46:18.180] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-007938)  
The answer, Mr. Speaker, Honorable Colleague, is yes.

**[06:46:24.120] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-007939)  
PhilHealth, together with the Philippine Institute for Development Studies, has developed a benefit prioritization framework to guide the evidence-based expansion of benefits.

**[06:46:36.160] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-007940)  
The framework prioritizes conditions with the highest disease burden and financial risk, ensuring that limited resources are directed.

**[06:46:48.480] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-007942)  
where they can deliver the greatest health, impact, and financial protection for our families.

**[06:51:36.920] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-007983)

**[06:51:39.300] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-007984)

**[06:51:46.040] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-007985)

**[06:51:55.760] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-007986)

**[06:52:02.900] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-007987)

**[06:52:10.080] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-007988)

**[06:52:36.660] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-007990)

**[06:56:14.780] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-008022)

**[06:56:23.100] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-008023)

**[07:36:08.060] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-009044)  
Ito po ay

pre-exposure

pre-exposure

prophylaxis.

**[07:55:57.160] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-009574)  
meron pong dalawang complaint

**[07:55:58.600] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-009575)  
na may kinalaman dito sa

**[07:56:02.080] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-009576)  
Anti-Hospital Deposit Law

**[07:56:04.240] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-009577)  
or RA 10932.

**[07:56:07.480] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-009578)  
So, dalawa sa 2024,

**[07:56:09.140] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-009579)  
anim sa 2025,

**[07:56:11.360] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-009580)  
at apat

**[07:56:12.240] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-009581)  
ngayong 2026.

**[07:56:16.980] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-009582)  
Parang sa akin pa lang po yata.

**[07:56:19.000] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-009583)  
Lumampas na yung reklamo dyan.

**[07:56:21.180] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-009584)  
Anyway,

**[07:56:23.880] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-009585)  
meron po bang

**[07:56:26.480] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-009586)  
the distinguished sponsor,

**[07:56:28.600] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-009587)  
Mr. Speaker, meron po ba tayong

**[07:56:30.520] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-009588)  
like a seal of good governance

**[07:56:32.360] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-009589)  
na ini-implement para sa mga

**[07:56:34.560] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-009590)  
hospital? Na parang

**[07:56:36.620] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-009591)  
nagkakaroon tayo ng rating sa kanila

**[07:56:38.580] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-009592)  
and when

**[07:56:40.360] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-009593)  
pag wala pong silang

**[07:56:41.820] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-009594)  
pag wala pong silang mga violation

**[07:56:44.660] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-009595)  
na ganito,

**[07:56:46.620] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-009596)  
parang

**[07:56:47.000] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-009597)  
mas maganda ang rating nila.

**[07:56:51.460] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-009598)  
And we can

**[07:56:52.520] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-009599)  
recommend them to

**[07:56:53.880] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-009600)  
patients.

**[07:56:58.600] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-009601)  
Meron pong

**[07:56:59.740] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-009602)  
kasalukuyan, existing

**[07:57:01.420] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-009603)  
star award, pero

**[07:57:03.520] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-009604)  
ayon sa ating

**[07:57:05.480] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-009605)  
sekretary ng DOH,

**[07:57:07.800] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-009606)  
i-improve pa para talagang

**[07:57:09.620] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-009607)  
makita yung performance

**[07:57:11.620] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-009608)  
ng hospital.

**[07:57:13.340] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-009609)  
Thank you for that, distinguished sponsor, Mr. Speaker.

**[07:57:16.340] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-009610)  
Let me move to

**[07:57:17.600] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-009611)  
yung 24-hour emergency

**[07:57:19.600] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-009612)  
care gap.

**[07:57:21.000] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-009613)  
If a patient is admitted, but

**[07:57:23.420] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-009614)  
medically ready for discharge in less

**[07:57:25.560] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-009615)  
than 24 hours,

**[07:57:28.480] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-009616)  
what

**[07:57:29.280] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-009617)  
PhilHealth benefit applies?

**[07:57:32.940] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-009618)  
We should not keep

**[07:57:34.600] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-009619)  
someone unnecessarily

**[07:57:37.000] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-009620)  
just to qualify

**[07:57:39.740] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-009621)  
for a benefit.

**[07:57:41.460] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-009622)  
So, ang tinatanong ko po dito,

**[07:57:45.140] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-009623)  
conversely,

**[07:57:46.500] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-009624)  
more than 24 hours

**[07:57:47.680] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-009625)  
nasa ER

**[07:57:48.360] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-009626)  
because the hospital is full,

**[07:57:52.120] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-009627)  
but technically, hindi

**[07:57:53.220] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-009628)  
admitted. So, ano pong benefit

**[07:57:55.080] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-009629)  
ang mag-a-apply?

**[07:57:57.400] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-009630)  
So, again,

**[07:57:59.520] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-009631)  
your timing is

**[07:58:01.200] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-009632)  
impeccable.

**[07:58:03.320] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-009633)  
Honorable colleague, Mr. Chair,

**[07:58:06.200] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-009634)  
PhilHealth already

**[07:58:07.280] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-009635)  
issued policy

**[07:58:08.760] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-009636)  
effective September

**[07:58:10.760] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-009637)  
18 with regards

**[07:58:13.380] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-009638)  
to admission less than

**[07:58:15.220] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-009639)  
24 hours, and

**[07:58:17.120] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-009640)  
that will now be

**[07:58:19.020] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-009641)  
covered. So, pag ikaw po

**[07:58:21.280] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-009642)  
ay na-ER, lumampas ka na

**[07:58:23.180] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-009643)  
ng 24 hours, pinaalis

**[07:58:25.180] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-009644)  
ka, dahil

**[07:58:26.860] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-009645)  
pwede ka na umalis, kaso

**[07:58:28.920] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-009646)  
pinaalis ka,

**[07:58:31.320] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-009647)  
pwede ka na mag-qualify.

**[07:58:35.240] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-009648)  
Tama po.

**[07:58:37.200] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-009649)  
And if you are

**[07:58:38.680] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-009650)  
admitted, if you nagpa-check-up ka,

**[07:58:42.340] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-009651)  
in-admit ka,

**[07:58:43.420] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-009652)  
pero pinalabas ka in less than

**[07:58:44.880] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-009653)  
24 hours, covered ka na rin.

**[07:58:48.800] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-009654)  
Tama po.

**[07:58:50.440] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-009655)  
Magandang balita po yan.

**[07:58:51.760] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-009656)  
Maraming salamat. Distinguished po.

**[07:58:53.180] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-009657)  
Sponsor, maraming salamat sa DOH.

**[07:58:56.600] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-009658)  
Let me move to my next question.

**[07:58:59.020] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-009659)  
Mr. Speaker, quick question lang for

**[07:59:01.040] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-009660)  
PhilHealth. Are you planning to

**[07:59:02.980] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-009661)  
expand benefits for diabetes

**[07:59:05.220] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-009662)  
and heart disease?

**[07:59:08.120] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-009663)  
Specifically, coverage

**[07:59:09.360] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-009664)  
for glucose monitoring devices

**[07:59:11.040] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-009665)  
for diabetic patients

**[07:59:13.100] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-009666)  
and increased coverage for cardiac devices

**[07:59:15.420] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-009667)  
such as pacemakers?

**[07:59:23.180] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-009668)  
Honorable colleague, Mr. Speaker,

**[07:59:30.640] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-009669)  
kasalukuyang pong

**[07:59:31.580] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-009670)  
pinag-aaralan ng PhilHealth,

**[07:59:33.520] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-009671)  
dahil medyo may kamahalan po yung mga

**[07:59:35.440] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-009672)  
devices na ito, pero dun po

**[07:59:37.620] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-009673)  
sa yakap at gamot package,

**[07:59:40.420] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-009674)  
pinoprovide na po yung mga

**[07:59:41.920] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-009675)  
insulin.

**[07:59:44.380] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-009676)  
Kaya po sa mga device,

**[07:59:46.080] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-009677)  
medyo may kamahalan.

**[07:59:46.440] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-009678)  
Distinguished colleague,

**[07:59:48.580] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-009679)  
these are concerns raised by

**[07:59:50.600] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-009680)  
the Philippine Society of Pediatric

**[07:59:52.720] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-009681)  
Metabolism and Endocrinology

**[07:59:55.260] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-009682)  
and the Philippine Heart

**[07:59:57.100] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-009683)  
Rhythm Society.

**[07:59:59.120] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-009684)  
Napag-aralan na po.

**[08:00:00.000] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-009685)  
Natanggap na po ng PhilHealth at kasalukuyang po ang pinag-aaralan.

**[08:00:08.560] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-009686)  
Kung sakali po bang magiging medically justified yan, magkano ang kakailanganin na additional funding ng PhilHealth para sa 2027?

**[08:00:19.380] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-009687)  
At this point, Mr. Speaker, Honorable Colleague, wala pang figure kasi kasalukuyang pinag-aaralan.

**[08:00:25.820] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-009688)  
But PhilHealth can update the Honorable Nassal on this.

**[08:00:30.360] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-009689)  
But it is a consideration. We are considering expanding the service.

**[08:00:37.880] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-009690)  
Yeah, well, pinag-aaralan po, Honorable Colleague.

**[08:00:42.380] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-009691)  
Thank you for that, Distinguished Sponsor, Mr. Speaker.

**[08:00:46.100] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-009692)  
May I move now to Yakap.

**[08:00:49.820] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-009693)  
To Yakap.

**[08:00:51.980] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-009694)  
Mr. Speaker, once PhilHealth pays an LGU for its services,

**[08:00:55.820] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-009695)  
Yakap patients, how do you ensure that money actually improves that health center?

**[08:01:03.680] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-009696)  
Doctors, medicines, laboratories, diagnostics, and equipment?

**[08:01:09.860] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-009697)  
Kasi po, di ba currently, parang nagmumawa lang yung mga LGU.

**[08:01:14.000] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-009698)  
So, ang tinatanong lang po natin is, is there an assurance na pag nabayaran na po sila, dumami yung PhilHealth, yung Yakap patients,

**[08:01:24.060] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-009699)  
how will we assure na ito pong mga LGUs will improve on their health centers na ginawa po nilang Yakap Center?

**[08:01:31.240] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-009700)  
Thank you.

**[08:01:44.220] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-009701)  
So, dun po sa mga accredited LGUs na qualified na po or accredited for the Yakap program,

**[08:01:51.980] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-009702)  
syempre may computation fund po ito.

**[08:01:54.040] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-009703)  
And, dapat lamang na yung LGU ginagamit ito to improve their facility.

**[08:02:01.360] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-009704)  
So, the question po, tama po yun.

**[08:02:03.960] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-009705)  
So, the question is, how do we assure that they will improve on the facility?

**[08:02:09.160] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-009706)  
Is there a change in the contract or the qualification?

**[08:02:13.420] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-009707)  
Kasi po, di ba, right now, we allow them to become Yakap, to be accredited as a Yakap facility

**[08:02:20.660] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-009708)  
on the condition that they...

**[08:02:24.040] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-009709)  
Have a MOA with either hospitals or diagnostic clinics.

**[08:02:32.960] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-009710)  
Tama po yun, yung mga primary health care centers, pwedeng mag-outsource habang wala pa sila na mga facilities na ito.

**[08:02:43.060] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-009711)  
So, how long will we allow that until we expect improvement from their own facilities?

**[08:02:52.620] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-009712)  
Originally, Mr. Speaker, Honorable Colleague, 2027 yung deadline.

**[08:02:59.060] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-009713)  
Pero, we understand, iba-iba yung kapasidad ng bawat LGU.

**[08:03:03.620] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-009714)  
Kaya, some may comply and some may lag behind, Mr. Speaker, Honorable Colleague.

**[08:03:14.420] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-009715)  
Mr. Speaker, my next question is, Distinguished Sponsor,

**[08:03:18.280] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-009716)  
do we monitor whether LGU Yakap facilities are actually issuing gamot prescriptions?

**[08:03:27.200] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-009717)  
Because kami po, nakikita namin na may patients who cannot access Yakap gamot

**[08:03:32.680] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-009718)  
because the LGU is waiting for its own Yakap gamot accreditation.

**[08:03:41.600] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-009719)  
Is an LGU allowed to delay or withhold a medically indicated prescription for that reason?

**[08:03:48.280] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-009720)  
So, opo, the answer is yes, Mr. Speaker, Honorable Colleague, minomonitor po.

**[08:03:58.960] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-009721)  
And in order to improve the system further, ngayon po, yung mga medicine, ililipat na po dun sa gamot program

**[08:04:08.900] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-009722)  
para to ensure na may constant and reliable supply because these are relationships with the pharmacy.

**[08:04:18.080] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-009723)  
Are we also looking into the possibility of not limiting the prescription coming from Yakap accredited doctors?

**[08:04:26.260] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-009724)  
From PhilHealth, including yung PhilHealth universe na po ng mga doctors?

**[08:04:32.740] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-009725)  
Currently po, kailangan dun sa accredited health provider.

**[08:04:37.980] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-009726)  
Hindi siya pwedeng coming from, tapos mag-avail sa mga accredited doctors ng PhilHealth,

**[08:04:45.820] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-009727)  
tapos mag-avail na lang sa Yakap.

**[08:04:48.080] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-009728)  
Pwede pong pag-aralan, pero para may control po tayo sa quality ng service na ibibigay at hindi magka-problema,

**[08:04:57.940] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-009729)  
yun po ngayon yung sistema.

**[08:05:00.700] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-009730)  
Thank you.

**[08:05:01.620] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-009731)  
Thank you for that.

**[08:05:03.060] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-009732)  
For everything, Mr. Sponsor.

**[08:05:04.860] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-009733)  
Thank you, Mr. Speaker.

**[08:05:06.400] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-009734)  
Maraming salamat po sa DOH and I fully support the budget for 2027.

**[08:05:10.780] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-009735)  
Thank you very much, Mr. Speaker.

**[08:05:13.460] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-009736)  
At maraming salamat sa ating kaibigan, si Honorable Robert Nazal.

**[08:05:18.940] Senior Deputy Speaker Ferdinand L. Hernandez** (asr-009737)  
Maraming maraming salamat, Congressman Robert Nazal of VH Partylist.

**[08:05:24.580] Senior Deputy Speaker Ferdinand L. Hernandez** (asr-009738)  
Majority Leader, please.

**[08:05:26.540] Rep. Alyssa Michaela "Mica" M. Gonzales** (asr-009739)  
Mr. Speaker, next to interpolate the sponsor, I move to recognize

**[08:05:30.300] Rep. Alyssa Michaela "Mica" M. Gonzales** (asr-009740)  
Representative Journey Jet V. Nisay of Pusong Pinoy Partylist.

**[08:05:37.440] Senior Deputy Speaker Ferdinand L. Hernandez** (asr-009741)  
Congressman Nisay, you have the floor, sir. Please proceed.

**[08:05:40.980] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-009742)  
Mr. Speaker, sa ating buting sponsor, mga ilang katanungan lamang po.

**[08:05:46.960] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-009743)  
Mr. Speaker,

**[08:05:48.080] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-009744)  
Mr. Sponsor,

**[08:05:49.580] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-009745)  
sa DOH budget briefing po ay na-erase na may 36 out of 82 provinces

**[08:05:54.660] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-009746)  
na walang medical facility managed by DOH.

**[08:05:59.020] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-009747)  
Mr. Speaker,

**[08:06:00.580] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-009748)  
kaugnay po nito may panukalang House Bill No. 11349

**[08:06:04.600] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-009749)  
o ang Tertiary Hospital in Every Province Act

**[08:06:07.880] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-009750)  
authored by no less than our Mr. Sponsor himself,

**[08:06:11.780] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-009751)  
kong Abet,

**[08:06:12.960] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-009752)  
kasama si na Minority Leader Nonoy Libanan,

**[08:06:16.000] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-009753)  
kong Gila Garcia,

**[08:06:17.100] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-009754)  
siya,

**[08:06:17.880] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-009755)  
kong Shin Gonzales,

**[08:06:19.660] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-009756)  
at ang inyong lingkod

**[08:06:20.500] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-009757)  
na naglalayong magkaroon ng at least one DOH supervised tertiary hospital

**[08:06:26.320] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-009758)  
sa ating bawat lalawigan.

**[08:06:29.440] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-009759)  
Ang ating tanong,

**[08:06:30.780] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-009760)  
ay suportado ba o susuportahan po ba ng Department of Health

**[08:06:33.860] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-009761)  
ang panukalang ito?

**[08:06:35.700] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-009762)  
At sa pananaw ng DOH,

**[08:06:37.900] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-009763)  
paano makakatulong ang pagkakaroon ng at least one DOH managed tertiary hospital

**[08:06:42.420] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-009764)  
in every province sa pagtugon ng geographic gaps

**[08:06:45.800] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-009765)  
sa access to health care?

**[08:06:46.940] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-009766)  
At sa pananaw ng DOH,

**[08:06:47.100] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-009767)  
at sa pananaw ng higher level government hospital services?

**[08:06:54.040] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-009768)  
Thank you for that question,

**[08:06:56.700] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-009769)  
Mr. Speaker,

**[08:06:58.300] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-009770)  
Honorable Nisay.

**[08:07:00.120] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-009771)  
Ang sagot po sa inyong katanungan is yes,

**[08:07:02.900] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-009772)  
especially dun sa 36 na wala pa pong DOH hospital na nakatalaga

**[08:07:09.700] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-009773)  
dun po sa probinsya.

**[08:07:13.160] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-009774)  
Mr. Speaker,

**[08:07:14.480] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-009775)  
sa atin pong butihing sponsor,

**[08:07:17.100] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-009783)
